Any existing WireGuard/Telegram/WhatsApp/HTTP-bridge/notification code anywhere in the repo (likely none — confirm), and any push-notification or remote-approval seam.\n\nDo NOT propose changes — just map what exists and concrete reuse points. {"parentUuid":"8246089c-13cf-4fb6-bd08-cab963de48e2","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a9a9282aa914a9b3f","attachment":{"type":"skill_listing","content":"- crucible: How to operate CRUCIBLE — the authorized, prove-don't-guess offensive-security engine in framework/v2. Read this BEFORE running an engagement/scan, re-verifying a finding, inspecting the event spine, reasoning about the veracity/anti-hallucination layer, the learning/metacognition core, or the safety stack. Use it when a task names CRUCIBLE, OBSIDIAN, `python3 -m framework.v2 <subcommand>`, findings/oracles/evidence certificates, the blackboard event spine, critics, reflection, or the reward/calibration loop.\n- deep-research: Deep research harness — fan-out web searches, fetch sources, adversarially verify claims, synthesize a cited report. - When the user wants a deep, multi-source, fact-checked research report on any topic. BEFORE invoking, check if the question is specific enough to research directly — if underspecified (e.g., \"what car to buy\" without budget/use-case/region), ask 2-3 clarifying questions to narrow scope. Then pass the refined question as args, weaving the answers in.\n- adversarial-sweep: Independent multi-lens adversarial re-attack of a defensive-oracle domain — the FORGE stage-9 companion to RED-PEN. Attack from disjoint lenses, refute each objection with a real repro, report only what survives. - Before merging any AEGIS/FORGE domain stream, alongside (not instead of) the red-pen agent. Pass the domain spec via args. On the email-auth stream it caught 3 CRITICAL/LOW defects PAST a RED-PEN PASS — near-zero-FP cannot be self-certified by one reviewer.\n- dataviz: Use this skill whenever you are about to create ANY chart, graph, plot, dashboard, or data visualization, in ANY output medium — an HTML or React artifact, inline SVG, plotting code in any library (matplotlib, plotly, d3, Recharts, …), an image/PNG you will render and upload, or a chart shared into Slack. Read it BEFORE writing the first line of chart code, choosing chart colors, building a stat tile / meter / KPI row, or laying out a dashboard. Produces visualizations that read as one system — elegant, accessible, consistent in light and dark — using a brand-neutral placeholder palette you swap for your own. Teaches a design-system-agnostic method: a form heuristic, a color formula with a runnable validator, mark specs, and interaction rules. A validated default palette is documented in `references/palette.md` — swap that file's values for your brand's. Triggers on: \"chart\", \"graph\", \"plot\", \"data viz\", \"visualization\", \"dashboard\", \"analytics\", \"visualize data\", \"categorical colors\", \"sequential / diverging palette\", \"stat tile\", \"sparkline\", \"heatmap\", \"legend\", \"axis\", \"tooltip\", \"chart colors\", \"color by series\".\n- artifact-design: Design guidance and fundamentals for Artifacts.\n- artifact-capabilities: Runtime capabilities a published Artifact page can be granted — behavior static HTML cannot provide on its own, such as the page reading live or connected data, keeping state shared across viewers, or updating and republishing itself. Serves this user's live capability roster and the typed call definitions. Load it whenever the user asks for an artifact needing any such runtime behavior.\n- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- verify: Verify that a code change actually does what it's supposed to by exercising it end-to-end and observing behavior — drive the affected flow, not just tests or typecheck. Run before committing nontrivial changes; bootstraps this repo's project verify skill if none exists yet. Don't invoke it on a diff that only touches tests, docs, or other code with no runtime surface to drive (a change to product source always has one) — there's nothing to observe.\n- code-review: Review the current diff for correctness bugs and reuse/simplification/efficiency cleanups at the given effort level (low/medium: fewer, high-confidence findings; high→max: broader coverage, may include uncertain findings; ultra: deep multi-agent review in the cloud). Pass --comment to post findings as inline PR comments, or --fix to apply the findings to the working tree after the review.\n- simplify: Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. Quality only — it does not hunt for bugs; use /code-review for that.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled cloud agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring cloud agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Reference for the Claude API / Anthropic SDK — model ids, pricing, params, streaming, tool use, MCP, agents, caching, token counting, model migration.\nTRIGGER — read BEFORE opening the target file; don't skip because it \"looks like a one-liner\" — whenever: the prompt names Claude/Anthropic in any form (Claude, Anthropic, Fable, Opus, Sonnet, Haiku, `anthropic`, `@anthropic-ai`, `claude-*`, `us.anthropic.*`, `[1m]`); the user asks about an LLM (pricing/model choice/limits/caching) — never answer from memory; OR the task is LLM-shaped with provider unstated (agent/MCP/tool-definition/multi-agent/RAG/LLM-judge/computer-use; generate/summarize/extract/classify/rewrite/converse over NL; debugging refusals/cutoffs/streaming/tool-calls/tokens).\nSKIP only when another provider is being worked on (overrides all triggers): OpenAI/GPT/Gemini/Llama/Mistral/Cohere/Ollama named in the query; OR `grep -rE 'openai|langchain_openai|google.generativeai|genai|mistralai|cohere|ollama'` over the project hits (run this grep FIRST if no provider named — don't Read the file).\n- run: Launch and drive this project's app to see a change working. {"parentUuid":"5d714686-c53e-4b72-8bea-1e84637f0600","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a9a9282aa914a9b3f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_017XTUQB3owQh8cAKia6fDTb","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The VLM seam (SIGIL §8 / C4-C6). A `VisionModel` turns a captured Frame + a question into a\n2\tfree-text visual reading. That reading is ADVISORY — the perception layer serves the frame's\n3\tcaptured TEXT as the authoritative answer and labels the model's reading as unverified (see\n4\t`perceive.compose_perception`). This mirrors the cascade doctrine: the frontier VLM is a tool\n5\tSIGIL CALLS, not the source of truth.\n6\t\n7\tHONEST GAP: a real VLM call needs either the metered Anthropic vision API or a local moondream-\n8\tclass model; neither is wired offline here. `ClaudeVision` is the documented real seam and returns\n9\t'' when no endpoint is configured (an absent reading is empty, never fabricated). Tests inject a\n10\tdeterministic double.\"\"\"\n11\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n12\t\n13\tfrom typing import Optional, Protocol, runtime_checkable\n14\t\n15\tfrom .capture import Frame\n16\t\n17\t\n18\t@runtime_checkable\n19\tclass VisionModel(Protocol):\n20\t    def describe(self, frame: Frame, question: str) -> str: ...   # advisory free-text reading\n21\t\n22\t\n23\tclass ClaudeVision:\n24\t    \"\"\"Documented real provider: base64 the frame → a frontier VLM (Anthropic vision API or an MCP\n25\t    image tool) → a short reading. Left inert offline: with no configured endpoint it returns ''\n26\t    so the perception answer stands on the captured text alone (never on a fabricated reading).\"\"\"\n27\t    def __init__(self, endpoint: Optional[str] = None, model: str = \"claude-sonnet-5\", timeout: int = 60):\n28\t        self.endpoint, self.model, self.timeout = endpoint, model, timeout\n29\t\n30\t    def describe(self, frame: Frame, question: str) -> str:\n31\t        if not self.endpoint or not frame.image_path:\n32\t            return \"\"   # no endpoint / no image → no reading (honest empty, not a guess)\n33\t        # A wired implementation would POST the base64 image + question to self.endpoint here.\n34\t        # Deliberately not faked: an unconfigured VLM must not manufacture a description.\n35\t        return \"\"\n36\t"}]},"uuid":"bafd87d8-dd87-4068-b252-7f4c185a5f71","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:22.557Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"5d714686-c53e-4b72-8bea-1e84637f0600","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"cf927ac2-1103-445d-b44c-d3fba3797c59","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a9a9282aa914a9b3f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01JpghDGrGZqANKVhhjdW5F9","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Capture backends (SIGIL §8). A `Frame` is the immutable record of one capture: the image bytes'\n2\tsha256 (integrity), the OCR/accessibility TEXT (the GROUND TRUTH a query is answered from), and a\n3\tpath to the image. Real backends shell out to whatever is installed (screenshot tool + tesseract,\n4\tv4l2/ffmpeg); every backend returns `None` on any failure — a missing camera is no capture, never a\n5\tfabricated one (same posture as SCHOLAR.read_source). `StaticFrame` is the deterministic test double.\n6\t\n7\tHONEST GAPS: headless hosts often lack a screenshot tool / tesseract / a camera, so the real\n8\tbackends degrade to `None` or an image-only Frame (text=\"\"). The richest real screen source is the\n9\tOS accessibility tree (C4); it is not available on a headless box and is left as a documented seam.\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\timport shutil\n13\timport subprocess\n14\timport tempfile\n15\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n16\tfrom pathlib import Path\n17\tfrom typing import Optional\n18\t\n19\tfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex\n20\t\n21\t_SCREENSHOT_TOOLS = (\n22\t    (\"scrot\", [\"scrot\", \"-o\"]),\n23\t    (\"gnome-screenshot\", [\"gnome-screenshot\", \"-f\"]),\n24\t    (\"spectacle\", [\"spectacle\", \"-b\", \"-n\", \"-o\"]),\n25\t    (\"import\", [\"import\", \"-window\", \"root\"]),   # ImageMagick\n26\t)\n27\t\n28\t\n29\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n30\tclass Frame:\n31\t    kind: str                       # \"screen\" | \"camera\"\n32\t    sha256: str                     # integrity of the captured image bytes\n33\t    text: str                       # OCR / accessibility text — the GROUND TRUTH; \"\" if none\n34\t    image_path: Optional[str] = None\n35\t    width: int = 0\n36\t    height: int = 0\n37\t\n38\t    @classmethod\n39\t    def from_image(cls, kind: str, image_path: str, *, text: str = \"\") -> \"Frame\":\n40\t        data = Path(image_path).read_bytes()\n41\t        return cls(kind=kind, sha256=sha256_hex(data), text=text, image_path=image_path)\n42\t\n43\t\n44\tdef _ocr(image_path: str) -> str:\n45\t    \"\"\"Extract text via tesseract if present; '' otherwise (no OCR is not a failure of capture).\"\"\"\n46\t    if not shutil.which(\"tesseract\"):\n47\t        return \"\"\n48\t    try:\n49\t        proc = subprocess.run([\"tesseract\", image_path, \"stdout\"],\n50\t                              capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)\n51\t        return (proc.stdout or \"\").strip()\n52\t    except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError):\n53\t        return \"\"\n54\t\n55\t\n56\tdef grab_screen() -> Optional[Frame]:\n57\t    \"\"\"Screenshot the current display + OCR it. Returns None if no screenshot tool is available\n58\t    or the grab fails. The accessibility-tree path (richer, C4) is a documented seam, not wired\n59\t    on headless hosts.\"\"\"\n60\t    out = tempfile.mktemp(suffix=\".png\", prefix=\"sigil-screen-\")\n61\t    for name, argv in _SCREENSHOT_TOOLS:\n62\t        if not shutil.which(name):\n63\t            continue\n64\t        try:\n65\t            proc = subprocess.run([*argv, out], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=20)\n66\t        except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError):\n67\t            continue\n68\t        if proc.returncode == 0 and Path(out).exists() and Path(out).stat().st_size > 0:\n69\t            return Frame.from_image(\"screen\", out, text=_ocr(out))\n70\t    return None\n71\t\n72\t\n73\tdef grab_camera(device: str = \"/dev/video0\") -> Optional[Frame]:\n74\t    \"\"\"Grab a single frame from a v4l2 camera via ffmpeg. Returns None if ffmpeg or the device is\n75\t    absent, or the grab fails. Camera frames rarely carry text, so OCR is best-effort.\"\"\"\n76\t    if not shutil.which(\"ffmpeg\") or not Path(device).exists():\n77\t        return None\n78\t    out = tempfile.mktemp(suffix=\".png\", prefix=\"sigil-cam-\")\n79\t    try:\n80\t        proc = subprocess.run(\n81\t            [\"ffmpeg\", \"-y\", \"-f\", \"v4l2\", \"-i\", device, \"-frames:v\", \"1\", out],\n82\t            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=20)\n83\t    except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError):\n84\t        return None\n85\t    if proc.returncode == 0 and Path(out).exists() and Path(out).stat().st_size > 0:\n86\t        return Frame.from_image(\"camera\", out, text=_ocr(out))\n87\t    return None\n88\t\n89\t\n90\tdef StaticFrame(*, kind: str = \"screen\", text: str = \"\", tag: str = \"\") -> Frame:\n91\t    \"\"\"Deterministic test/double frame — no filesystem, sha256 derived from (tag or text) so\n92\t    identical content hashes identically (drives ambient change-detection).\"\"\"\n93\t    return Frame(kind=kind, sha256=sha256_hex((tag or text).encode()), text=text, image_path=None)\n94\t"}]},"uuid":"9e8989f2-82f1-462a-8b20-ad98e14a2056","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:22.561Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"cf927ac2-1103-445d-b44c-d3fba3797c59","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"4384062c-0841-43b6-8f54-9311fef33e2d","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a9a9282aa914a9b3f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01EeEcbVwgPqCG9qPKwzu18r","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Perceptor (SIGIL §4.3/§8, C4-C6) — turns a captured Frame into a GROUNDED perception answer and\n2\twrites it as an event record. Ceiling A1 (observe; writes only event records).\n3\t\n4\tTHE DISCIPLINE (serve-the-quote, reused from SCHOLAR): the frame's captured TEXT is the\n5\tauthoritative content of the answer — verbatim, what the OCR/accessibility layer actually read off\n6\tthe owner's screen. The VLM's visual reading is ADVISORY and labelled as unverified, so a divergent\n7\tor fabricated reading can never be presented as what is on the screen. If no text was captured\n8\t(image-only camera frame), the answer says so and the reading is explicitly the only, unverified,\n9\tsignal — never dressed up as fact.\n10\t\n11\tAmbient vision (C6) is OPT-IN: `ambient_watch` establishes a baseline frame then escalates (runs the\n12\tVLM, writes an event) ONLY when a later frame differs. Unchanged frames never leave the machine and\n13\tnothing persists beyond the event records.\"\"\"\n14\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n15\t\n16\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n17\t\n18\tfrom ..agents.base import Agent, AgentResult, Proposal, Tier\n19\tfrom .capture import Frame\n20\tfrom .vision import VisionModel\n21\t\n22\t\n23\t# Section headers rendered at COLUMN 0. Captured screen text is guard-prefixed (see below) so it can\n24\t# never occupy column 0 — therefore an EXACT-LINE match on these headers is an unforgeable boundary,\n25\t# even when attacker-controlled screen content contains the header string verbatim (red-pen REDPEN-P5-2).\n26\tAUTHORITATIVE_HEADER = \"## On-screen text (authoritative — captured verbatim from the frame)\"\n27\tADVISORY_HEADER = \"## Model's visual reading (ADVISORY — a guess about the image, NOT verified against captured text)\"\n28\t_GUARD = \"  │ \"   # every captured line starts here → it can never impersonate a column-0 header\n29\t\n30\t\n31\tdef compose_perception(question: str, frame: Frame, reading: str) -> str:\n32\t    \"\"\"Serve the captured text as authoritative; the VLM reading as ADVISORY. Never merge them.\n33\t    The authoritative/advisory boundary is the column-0 header line — unforgeable because every\n34\t    captured line is guard-prefixed. Machine consumers should key off the structured payload fields\n35\t    (`captured_text` vs `vision_reading_advisory`); this rendering is the human-readable view.\"\"\"\n36\t    lines = [f\"# Perception — {question or '(observe)'}\", \"\"]\n37\t    captured = (frame.text or \"\").strip()\n38\t    if captured:\n39\t        lines.append(AUTHORITATIVE_HEADER)\n40\t        for ln in captured.splitlines():\n41\t            if ln.strip():\n42\t                lines.append(f\"{_GUARD}{ln.rstrip()}\")   # guard-prefixed: cannot forge the boundary\n43\t        lines.append(f\"\\n(frame: {frame.kind}, sha256 {frame.sha256[:12]}, {len(captured)} chars captured)\")\n44\t    else:\n45\t        lines.append(\"## No text captured from the frame\")\n46\t        lines.append(f\"(frame: {frame.kind}, sha256 {frame.sha256[:12]}, image-only — nothing OCR-grounded to serve)\")\n47\t    lines.append(\"\")\n48\t    lines.append(ADVISORY_HEADER)\n49\t    lines.append(f\"- {reading.strip() if reading and reading.strip() else '(no reading available)'}\")\n50\t    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n51\t\n52\t\n53\tclass Perceptor(Agent):\n54\t    name = \"PERCEPTION\"\n55\t    mandate = \"answer screen/camera queries from captured ground truth; VLM reading is advisory\"\n56\t    ceiling = Tier.A1\n57\t\n58\t    def perceive(self, question: str, frame: Frame, *, vision: Optional[VisionModel] = None) -> AgentResult:\n59\t        reading = \"\"\n60\t        if vision is not None:\n61\t            try:\n62\t                reading = vision.describe(frame, question) or \"\"\n63\t            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — a failing VLM yields no reading, never a crash/guess\n64\t                reading = \"\"\n65\t        text = compose_perception(question, frame, reading)\n66\t        # The one-line summary must NEVER be a bare VLM reading presented as fact: when there is no\n67\t        # captured text, either label the reading unverified or fall back to a neutral placeholder\n68\t        # (red-pen RP-PERCEPT-01 / REDPEN-P5-1). A grounded summary is the captured text only.\n69\t        captured = frame.text.strip()\n70\t        if captured:\n71\t            summary = captured[:120]\n72\t        elif reading.strip():\n73\t            summary = f\"(unverified VLM reading) {reading.strip()[:100]}\"\n74\t        else:\n75\t            summary = f\"({frame.kind} image, no text)\"\n76\t        return self._dispatch([Proposal(\"event\", {\n77\t            \"signal\": \"perception\",\n78\t            \"subject\": question or \"observe\",\n79\t            \"summary\": summary,\n80\t            \"text\": text,\n81\t            \"frame_kind\": frame.kind,\n82\t            \"frame_sha256\": frame.sha256,\n83\t            \"captured_text\": frame.text,               # authoritative ground truth\n84\t            \"vision_reading_advisory\": reading,        # advisory only\n85\t            \"grounded\": bool(frame.text.strip()),\n86\t        }, Tier.A0)])   # observe → A0, auto\n87\t\n88\t    def ambient_watch(self, frames: List[Frame], *, vision: Optional[VisionModel] = None) -> tuple:\n89\t        \"\"\"Opt-in ambient loop (C6). Establish a baseline, then ESCALATE only on a changed frame.\n90\t        Returns (escalation_count, [AgentResult]). Emits indicator start/stop markers; unchanged\n91\t        frames produce nothing (they never leave the machine).\n92\t\n93\t        Change = BYTE-IDENTITY of the captured frame (sha256). This is a deliberately coarse baseline:\n94\t        a real deployment swaps in a perceptual-hash / small-VLM delta so lighting jitter isn't a\n95\t        change and a meaningful scene shift is — the escalation LOGIC here is what's under test, not\n96\t        the sophistication of the delta metric.\"\"\"\n97\t        self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n98\t                          payload={\"signal\": \"perception.ambient\", \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\",\n99\t                                   \"summary\": \"ambient watch STARTED (indicator lit — opt-in)\"})\n100\t        last: Optional[Frame] = None\n101\t        escalations: List[AgentResult] = []\n102\t        for fr in frames:\n103\t            if last is None:\n104\t                last = fr                      # baseline: no escalation, nothing leaves the machine\n105\t                continue\n106\t            if fr.sha256 == last.sha256:\n107\t                continue                       # no change → suppressed\n108\t            last = fr\n109\t            escalations.append(self.perceive(\"(ambient: scene changed)\", fr, vision=vision))\n110\t        self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n111\t                          payload={\"signal\": \"perception.ambient\", \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\",\n112\t                                   \"summary\": f\"ambient watch STOPPED — {len(escalations)} escalation(s)\"})\n113\t        return len(escalations), escalations\n114\t"}]},"uuid":"b078bae1-14e7-45fa-acab-22aa4dfd2dd9","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:22.570Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"4384062c-0841-43b6-8f54-9311fef33e2d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"5ba431f2-20c2-4196-9c0c-1270f469d134","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a9a9282aa914a9b3f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Nrfj1sCLcsLq2yNagdPHKA","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"SIGIL Perception subsystem (Phase 5, SIGIL §8). On-demand screen/camera capture → a grounded\n2\tperception answer. The DISCIPLINE mirrors SCHOLAR's serve-the-quote gate: the CAPTURED TEXT\n3\t(accessibility tree / OCR) is the AUTHORITATIVE ground truth; the VLM's visual reading is\n4\tADVISORY only, never asserted as the screen's content. Ambient vision (C6) is opt-in, indicator-\n5\tlit, and escalates only on a detected change — nothing persists beyond event records.\n6\t\n7\tOffense-free by doctrine: perception observes the owner's OWN screen/camera; it takes no\n8\texternal action (ceiling A0/A1).\"\"\"\n9\tfrom ..reuse import assert_no_offense\n10\t\n11\tassert_no_offense()\n12\t\n13\tfrom .capture import Frame, grab_camera, grab_screen  # noqa: E402\n14\tfrom .perceive import Perceptor, compose_perception  # noqa: E402\n15\tfrom .vision import ClaudeVision, VisionModel  # noqa: E402\n16\t\n17\t__all__ = [\"Frame\", \"grab_screen\", \"grab_camera\", \"Perceptor\", \"compose_perception\",\n18\t           \"VisionModel\", \"ClaudeVision\"]\n19\t"}]},"uuid":"5b5b5dc6-2203-4f64-8e6d-1bcd0bb02157","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:22.572Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"5ba431f2-20c2-4196-9c0c-1270f469d134","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"03f40ea7-e219-48fc-bdc1-189d2cabea99","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a9a9282aa914a9b3f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_011LXvt6w81YFd4jYdSJdmZ2","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"ARTIFICER (SIGIL §4.4) — engineering: drives headless Claude Code against a repo to own a\n2\tcoding task end to end. Ceiling A2 (a PR ready-for-review queues for approval); `git push` to a\n3\tprotected branch, deploys, and dependency additions are A3 — ARTIFICER NEVER pushes.\n4\t\n5\tCorrectness discipline (the §4.4 focus): it runs the tests BEFORE claiming done — a PR is\n6\tproposed ONLY if the test command passes on the change; a failing change is reported, not\n7\tshipped. The result is a local branch + a plain-language diff summary, QUEUED for the human to\n8\treview and push. The coder is pluggable: `ClaudeCoder` (headless `claude -p`) or a deterministic\n9\ttest double.\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\timport re\n13\timport shutil\n14\timport subprocess\n15\timport tempfile\n16\tfrom pathlib import Path\n17\tfrom typing import List, Optional, Protocol, runtime_checkable\n18\t\n19\tfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex\n20\tfrom .base import Agent, AgentResult, Proposal, Tier\n21\t\n22\t# commands that \"pass\" trivially — a PR must never claim tests passing on one of these.\n23\t_TRIVIAL_TEST = frozenset({(), (\"true\",), (\":\",), (\"/bin/true\",), (\"/usr/bin/true\",)})\n24\t\n25\t\n26\tdef _git(repo: str, *args: str, check: bool = True) -> str:\n27\t    proc = subprocess.run([\"git\", \"-C\", repo, *args], capture_output=True, text=True)\n28\t    if check and proc.returncode != 0:\n29\t        raise RuntimeError(f\"git {' '.join(args)} failed: {proc.stderr.strip()}\")\n30\t    return proc.stdout\n31\t\n32\t\n33\tdef _slug(task: str) -> str:\n34\t    base = re.sub(r\"[^a-z0-9]+\", \"-\", task.lower()).strip(\"-\")[:28] or \"task\"\n35\t    return f\"{base}-{sha256_hex(task.encode())[:6]}\"   # unique per task, stable\n36\t\n37\t\n38\t@runtime_checkable\n39\tclass Coder(Protocol):\n40\t    def code(self, task: str, workdir: str) -> str: ...   # perform the change; return a summary\n41\t\n42\t\n43\tclass ClaudeCoder:\n44\t    \"\"\"Headless Claude Code (`claude -p`) run inside the repo — the real ARTIFICER worker.\"\"\"\n45\t    def __init__(self, claude_bin: str = \"/home/kali/.local/bin/claude\", timeout: int = 600):\n46\t        self.claude_bin, self.timeout = claude_bin, timeout\n47\t\n48\t    def code(self, task: str, workdir: str) -> str:\n49\t        prompt = (f\"You are working in the repo at {workdir}. Task: {task}\\n\"\n50\t                  \"Make the minimal change to accomplish it. Do not run git. Do not touch unrelated files.\")\n51\t        # acceptEdits lets the headless coder actually apply Edit/Write in its sandboxed repo (it\n52\t        # cannot prompt for permission in -p mode). ARTIFICER's isolation is the per-task branch;\n53\t        # git push / deploy stay A3 and are never invoked here.\n54\t        try:\n55\t            proc = subprocess.run(\n56\t                [self.claude_bin, \"-p\", prompt, \"--permission-mode\", \"acceptEdits\"],\n57\t                cwd=workdir, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=self.timeout)\n58\t        except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError) as e:\n59\t            return f\"(coder error: {e})\"\n60\t        return (proc.stdout or \"\").strip()[:400]\n61\t\n62\t\n63\tclass Artificer(Agent):\n64\t    name = \"ARTIFICER\"\n65\t    mandate = \"own background coding tasks: tests before done, PRs not pushes\"\n66\t    ceiling = Tier.A2\n67\t\n68\t    def run(self, task: str, *, repo: str, test_cmd: Optional[List[str]] = None,\n69\t            coder: Optional[Coder] = None, branch: Optional[str] = None) -> AgentResult:\n70\t        coder = coder or ClaudeCoder()\n71\t        base = _git(repo, \"rev-parse\", \"--abbrev-ref\", \"HEAD\").strip()\n72\t        branch = branch or f\"artificer/{_slug(task)}\"\n73\t\n74\t        # ISOLATION: the coder works in a dedicated git WORKTREE off `base`, so the real working\n75\t        # tree is never touched, no pre-existing/unrelated edits get bundled, and a failure leaves\n76\t        # the main repo exactly as it was. The per-task branch survives for the human to review.\n77\t        _git(repo, \"worktree\", \"prune\", check=False)\n78\t        wt = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=\"artificer-wt-\")\n79\t        _git(repo, \"worktree\", \"add\", \"-B\", branch, wt, base)\n80\t        try:\n81\t            summary = coder.code(task, wt)\n82\t            changed = bool(_git(wt, \"status\", \"--porcelain\", check=False).strip())\n83\t            diff = _git(wt, \"diff\", \"--stat\", check=False).strip()\n84\t\n85\t            if not changed:\n86\t                res = AgentResult(agent=self.name)\n87\t                res.notes.append(\"no change produced for the task — nothing to propose.\")\n88\t                return res\n89\t\n90\t            real_test = test_cmd is not None and tuple(test_cmd) not in _TRIVIAL_TEST\n91\t            if not real_test:\n92\t                # NEVER claim \"tests passing\" without a real test command — record UNVERIFIED, withhold PR.\n93\t                res = self._dispatch([Proposal(\"finding\", {\n94\t                    \"summary\": f\"ARTIFICER task '{task}': change made but NO real test command was run — UNVERIFIED, PR withheld\",\n95\t                    \"branch\": branch, \"diffstat\": diff, \"coder\": summary}, Tier.A1)])\n96\t                res.notes.append(\"no real test command → change is UNVERIFIED; PR withheld (correctness discipline)\")\n97\t                return res\n98\t\n99\t            test = subprocess.run(test_cmd, cwd=wt, capture_output=True, text=True)\n100\t            if test.returncode != 0:\n101\t                tail = (test.stdout + test.stderr).strip().splitlines()\n102\t                res = self._dispatch([Proposal(\"finding\", {\n103\t                    \"summary\": f\"ARTIFICER task '{task}': change made but tests FAIL — PR withheld\",\n104\t                    \"branch\": branch, \"test_tail\": tail[-3:] if tail else [], \"diffstat\": diff}, Tier.A1)])\n105\t                res.notes.append(\"tests failed after the change — PR withheld (correctness discipline)\")\n106\t                return res\n107\t\n108\t            # tests genuinely pass → commit on the branch (in the worktree), propose a PR (A2, QUEUED).\n109\t            _git(wt, \"add\", \"-A\")\n110\t            _git(wt, \"commit\", \"-m\", f\"ARTIFICER: {task}\", check=False)\n111\t            res = self._dispatch([Proposal(\"pr\", {\n112\t                \"subject\": task, \"branch\": branch, \"base\": base, \"diffstat\": diff,\n113\t                \"summary\": summary, \"tests\": f\"passing ({' '.join(test_cmd)})\",\n114\t                \"note\": \"ready-for-review; ARTIFICER does NOT push — a human reviews and pushes\"}, Tier.A2)])\n115\t            res.notes.append(f\"task done, tests PASS → PR proposed on branch '{branch}' (A2, awaiting approval)\")\n116\t            return res\n117\t        finally:\n118\t            _git(repo, \"worktree\", \"remove\", \"--force\", wt, check=False)\n119\t            shutil.rmtree(wt, ignore_errors=True)\n120\t"}]},"uuid":"2865fe98-029b-4f0c-bb62-899fa3418600","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:27.219Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"03f40ea7-e219-48fc-bdc1-189d2cabea99","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"3596720b-70cb-417d-b675-e6bb4500f6e7","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a9a9282aa914a9b3f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Apxr18CTHth1Jr4QaZjpjy","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"SCHOLAR (SIGIL §4.5) — research & analysis: long-horizon research, sourced synthesis. Ceiling\n2\tA1 (research never touches external state). Its focus is EPISTEMICS: every claim carries a\n3\tsource and a verbatim quote, confidence is explicit, and a claim that does NOT verify against its\n4\tcited source is DEMOTED (not asserted) — the same serve-the-quote gate that governs the\n5\tconsolidation, reused here. The output is a `report` record grounded in real sources, so future\n6\trecall of the research is cited and honest, not confident. The synthesizer is pluggable\n7\t(`ClaudeSynthesizer` via `claude -p`, or a deterministic double).\"\"\"\n8\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n9\t\n10\timport json\n11\timport re\n12\timport subprocess\n13\tfrom typing import Dict, List, Optional, Protocol, runtime_checkable\n14\t\n15\tfrom ..consolidate.gate import salient   # reuse the veracity tokenizer\n16\tfrom .base import Agent, AgentResult, Proposal, Tier\n17\tfrom .sources import read_source\n18\t\n19\t_WS = re.compile(r\"\\s+\")\n20\t_MIN_QUOTE_SALIENT = 2\n21\t\n22\t\n23\tdef _norm(t: str) -> str:\n24\t    return _WS.sub(\" \", (t or \"\")).strip().lower()\n25\t\n26\t\n27\tdef grounds_in_source(quote: str, source_text: str) -> bool:\n28\t    \"\"\"A claim's quote must be verbatim in the cited source AND specific (≥2 salient tokens) —\n29\t    exactly the serve-the-quote discipline, applied to research sources.\"\"\"\n30\t    q = _norm(quote)\n31\t    if not q or len(salient(quote)) < _MIN_QUOTE_SALIENT:\n32\t        return False\n33\t    return q in _norm(source_text)\n34\t\n35\t\n36\t@runtime_checkable\n37\tclass Synthesizer(Protocol):\n38\t    def synthesize(self, question: str, docs: Dict[str, str]) -> List[dict]: ...  # [{claim,source,quote,confidence}]\n39\t\n40\t\n41\tclass ClaudeSynthesizer:\n42\t    \"\"\"`claude -p` over the sources → JSON claims, each citing a source ref + a verbatim quote.\"\"\"\n43\t    def __init__(self, claude_bin: str = \"/home/kali/.local/bin/claude\",\n44\t                 model: str = \"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001\", timeout: int = 180):\n45\t        self.claude_bin, self.model, self.timeout = claude_bin, model, timeout\n46\t\n47\t    def synthesize(self, question: str, docs: Dict[str, str]) -> List[dict]:\n48\t        rendered = \"\\n\\n\".join(f\"[SOURCE {ref}]\\n{text[:4000]}\" for ref, text in docs.items())\n49\t        prompt = (\n50\t            \"You are a research analyst. Answer the QUESTION using ONLY the SOURCES. For every claim, \"\n51\t            \"cite the exact source ref and copy a VERBATIM quote from it that supports the claim; if a \"\n52\t            \"source does not support a claim, do not make it. If sources DISAGREE, report both. Return \"\n53\t            'ONLY a JSON array: [{\"claim\":\"...\",\"source\":\"<ref>\",\"quote\":\"<verbatim>\",\"confidence\":<0..1>}].'\n54\t            f\"\\n\\nQUESTION: {question}\\n\\nSOURCES:\\n{rendered}\\n\"\n55\t        )\n56\t        try:\n57\t            proc = subprocess.run([self.claude_bin, \"-p\", prompt, \"--model\", self.model],\n58\t                                  capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=self.timeout)\n59\t        except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError):\n60\t            return []\n61\t        m = re.search(r\"\\[.*\\]\", proc.stdout, re.S)\n62\t        if not m:\n63\t            return []\n64\t        try:\n65\t            items = json.loads(m.group(0))\n66\t        except json.JSONDecodeError:\n67\t            return []\n68\t        out = []\n69\t        for it in items if isinstance(items, list) else []:\n70\t            if isinstance(it, dict) and it.get(\"claim\") and it.get(\"source\"):\n71\t                out.append({\"claim\": str(it[\"claim\"]), \"source\": str(it[\"source\"]),\n72\t                            \"quote\": str(it.get(\"quote\", \"\")), \"confidence\": float(it.get(\"confidence\", 0.5) or 0.5)})\n73\t        return out\n74\t\n75\t\n76\tdef compose_report(question: str, graded: List[dict], sources: List[str]) -> str:\n77\t    grounded = [g for g in graded if g[\"grounded\"]]\n78\t    lines = [f\"# SCHOLAR research — {question}\", \"\",\n79\t             f\"Sources consulted: {len(sources)}. Claims: {len(graded)} \"\n80\t             f\"({len(grounded)} source-verified, {len(graded) - len(grounded)} unverified).\", \"\"]\n81\t    if grounded:\n82\t        # SERVE THE QUOTE, not the model's claim: the authoritative content is the verbatim source\n83\t        # span (which is what actually verified). The model's claim is advisory ONLY — a fabricated\n84\t        # claim paired with a real quote must NOT be presented as a source-verified fact.\n85\t        lines.append(\"## Source-verified spans (the authoritative evidence, verbatim)\")\n86\t        for g in sorted(grounded, key=lambda x: -x[\"confidence\"]):\n87\t            lines.append(f\"- \\\"{g['quote'][:200]}\\\"\")\n88\t            lines.append(f\"    — {g['source']}  ·  model's reading (ADVISORY, not verified): \"\n89\t                         f\"{g['claim'][:120]}  [{g['confidence']:.0%}]\")\n90\t    ungrounded = [g for g in graded if not g[\"grounded\"]]\n91\t    if ungrounded:\n92\t        lines.append(\"\\n## Unverified (the model asserted these but no verbatim source span backs them — NOT relied upon)\")\n93\t        for g in ungrounded:\n94\t            lines.append(f\"- {g['claim'][:120]}  (claimed source {g['source']})\")\n95\t    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n96\t\n97\t\n98\tclass Scholar(Agent):\n99\t    name = \"SCHOLAR\"\n100\t    mandate = \"sourced research; claims carry sources + confidence; disagreement reported, not averaged\"\n101\t    ceiling = Tier.A1\n102\t\n103\t    def run(self, question: str, sources: List[str], *, synthesizer: Optional[Synthesizer] = None) -> AgentResult:\n104\t        docs = {ref: read_source(ref) for ref in sources}\n105\t        claims = (synthesizer or ClaudeSynthesizer()).synthesize(question, docs)\n106\t        graded = [{**c, \"grounded\": grounds_in_source(c.get(\"quote\", \"\"), docs.get(c[\"source\"], \"\"))}\n107\t                  for c in claims]\n108\t        text = compose_report(question, graded, sources)\n109\t        g = sum(1 for x in graded if x[\"grounded\"])\n110\t        res = self._dispatch([Proposal(\"report\", {\n111\t            \"subject\": question, \"text\": text, \"sources\": sources,\n112\t            \"claims\": len(graded), \"grounded\": g}, Tier.A1)])\n113\t        res.notes.append(f\"researched '{question[:50]}' over {len(sources)} source(s): \"\n114\t                         f\"{g}/{len(graded)} claims source-verified\")\n115\t        return res\n116\t"}]},"uuid":"b5beeb18-332a-4bec-950a-3a6cfbfcc19b","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:27.764Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"3596720b-70cb-417d-b675-e6bb4500f6e7","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"b25489b6-9564-482e-a794-6c5fbddb67de","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a9a9282aa914a9b3f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01JtmPEVVbUvutdchqizZ1qj","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"SIGIL runtime configuration. All paths local-first, owner-only.\"\"\"\n2\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n3\t\n4\timport os\n5\tfrom pathlib import Path\n6\t\n7\t# --- data root (runtime, 0700) --------------------------------------------------------\n8\tSIGIL_HOME = Path(os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_HOME\", str(Path.home() / \".sigil\")))\n9\t\n10\t\n11\tdef _load_env_file() -> None:\n12\t    \"\"\"Load persisted `KEY=VALUE` settings from ~/.sigil/sigil.env so a value set once\n13\t    (e.g. SIGIL_QDRANT_URL for server mode) reaches BOTH the CLI and the MCP server that\n14\t    Claude spawns without an explicit env. The real environment always wins (setdefault).\"\"\"\n15\t    f = SIGIL_HOME / \"sigil.env\"\n16\t    if not f.exists():\n17\t        return\n18\t    for line in f.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").splitlines():\n19\t        line = line.strip()\n20\t        if not line or line.startswith(\"#\") or \"=\" not in line:\n21\t            continue\n22\t        k, _, v = line.partition(\"=\")\n23\t        os.environ.setdefault(k.strip(), v.strip())\n24\t\n25\t\n26\t_load_env_file()\n27\t\n28\tSPINE_PATH = SIGIL_HOME / \"spine\" / \"spine.jsonl\"\n29\tHEAD_PATH = SIGIL_HOME / \"spine\" / \"head.json\"\n30\tKEYS_DIR = SIGIL_HOME / \"spine\" / \"keys\"\n31\tCACHE_DIR = SIGIL_HOME / \"cache\"\n32\t\n33\t# --- ingestion sources ----------------------------------------------------------------\n34\tCLAUDE_PROJECTS = Path(os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_CLAUDE_PROJECTS\", str(Path.home() / \".claude\" / \"projects\")))\n35\t# Phase-0a thin slice: PENTEST-main only. Slugified cwd = dir name under ~/.claude/projects.\n36\tPROJECT_ALLOWLIST = os.environ.get(\n37\t    \"SIGIL_PROJECT_ALLOWLIST\", \"-home-kali-Pictures-PENTEST-main\"\n38\t).split(\",\")\n39\t# ephemeral CRUCIBLE-engine runs to filter out\n40\tEPHEMERAL_PREFIXES = (\"-tmp-crucible-cc-\", \"-tmp-\")\n41\t\n42\t# --- vectors (Qdrant, local/embedded — no server, no Docker; in-process, file-backed) -\n43\tQDRANT_PATH = SIGIL_HOME / \"qdrant\"                       # embedded local-mode store\n44\tQDRANT_URL = os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_QDRANT_URL\", \"\")       # set to switch to a server later\n45\tQDRANT_COLLECTION = os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_QDRANT_COLLECTION\", \"sigil_memory\")\n46\tEMBED_MODEL = os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_EMBED_MODEL\", \"BAAI/bge-small-en-v1.5\")\n47\tEMBED_DIM = 384\n48\t\n49\t# --- graph (Kùzu, deterministic mirror of the spine) ----------------------------------\n50\tGRAPH_DIR = SIGIL_HOME / \"graph\"                          # base; holds current/ + staging/ (atomic swap)\n51\t\n52\t# --- identity / scope -----------------------------------------------------------------\n53\tSCOPE = os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_SCOPE\", \"sigil\")          # the owner's single scope\n54\tOWNER_KEY_ID = os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_OWNER_KEY_ID\", \"owner\")\n55\t\n56\t\n57\tdef ensure_dirs() -> None:\n58\t    for d in (SIGIL_HOME, SPINE_PATH.parent, KEYS_DIR, CACHE_DIR):\n59\t        d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n60\t        try:\n61\t            os.chmod(d, 0o700)\n62\t        except OSError:\n63\t            pass\n64\t"}]},"uuid":"018c5fef-6e8d-452e-a405-29ec93e53047","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:28.161Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b25489b6-9564-482e-a794-6c5fbddb67de","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

An ExtractionProvider turns a\n2\twindow of spine records into candidate facts. Three implementations:\n3\t\n4\t  * AgentProvider   — headless `claude -p` on the Max plan (D5: agent-driven, zero API cost).\n5\t  * ReplayProvider  — replays a captured golden fixture (zero cost, exercises the parse path).\n6\t  * HeuristicProvider — offline keyword extraction (no LLM; a weak fallback + deterministic\n7\t                        double for tests).\n8\t\n9\tNone of these is trusted: whatever a provider proposes is only a CANDIDATE. The gate\n10\t(gate.admit) re-executes every citation before anything becomes a fact. The extraction prompt\n11\tstill injects the prove-don't-guess doctrine so the agent is asked to cite verbatim and refuse\n12\tto invent — belt (honest prompt) and suspenders (re-execution gate).\"\"\"\n13\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n14\t\n15\timport json\n16\timport re\n17\timport subprocess\n18\tfrom pathlib import Path\n19\tfrom typing import Iterable, Protocol, runtime_checkable\n20\t\n21\tfrom ..spine.models import SpineRecord\n22\tfrom .models import CandidateFact\n23\t\n24\t# the prove-don't-guess doctrine, distilled for extraction (framework/cognitive/metacognition.md).\n25\tDOCTRINE = (\n26\t    \"You extract DURABLE FACTS from a person's work log. A `decision` is a CHOICE the person \"\n27\t    \"COMMITTED TO ('we'll use X', 'decided to Y') — NOT a status update, test result, log line, \"\n28\t    \"or an instruction to someone else. A `commitment` is a PROMISE the person made, ideally with a \"\n29\t    \"due date. An `entity` is a durable named thing (project/person/tool/component). If a record is \"\n30\t    \"just narration, a result, or a task assignment, extract NOTHING from it. Rules, above all else:\\n\"\n31\t    \"1. PROVE, DON'T GUESS. Only report a fact that the provided records EXPLICITLY support. \"\n32\t    \"Never infer, embellish, or invent a decision, commitment, or entity that is not in the text.\\n\"\n33\t    \"2. CITE VERBATIM. For every fact, cite the exact record seq(s) and copy a `quote` that is a \"\n34\t    \"VERBATIM substring of a cited record. If you cannot quote it word-for-word, do not report it. \"\n35\t    \"The `statement` must be a COMPRESSION of your quote — use ONLY words that appear in the quote \"\n36\t    \"(you may drop and reorder words, but never add a word the quote does not contain).\\n\"\n37\t    \"3. REFUSE HONESTLY. If a record supports no durable fact, return fewer facts — an empty list \"\n38\t    \"is a correct answer. Do not pad.\\n\"\n39\t    \"4. DON'T FABRICATE CONFIDENCE. `confidence` is your rough estimate only; it never makes a \"\n40\t    \"weakly-supported claim strong.\\n\"\n41\t    \"5. CONTRADICTIONS. If the records show the person REVERSING an earlier decision (choosing X \"\n42\t    \"then later choosing not-X or a different option for the SAME subject), emit a kind='contradiction' \"\n43\t    \"whose source_seqs cite BOTH the earlier and later records AND provide a `quotes` array with a \"\n44\t    \"VERBATIM quote from EACH of those two records. Only flag genuine OPPOSITION, never a restatement \"\n45\t    \"or refinement of the same choice.\\n\"\n46\t)\n47\t\n48\t_SCHEMA_HINT = (\n49\t    \"Return ONLY a JSON array (no prose) of objects: \"\n50\t    '{\"kind\": \"decision\"|\"commitment\"|\"entity\"|\"contradiction\", \"subject\": \"<what it is about>\", '\n51\t    '\"statement\": \"<advisory summary in the person\\'s terms>\", \"quote\": \"<verbatim span from a cited record>\", '\n52\t    '\"quotes\": [\"<verbatim from record A>\", \"<verbatim from record B>\"] (contradictions ONLY, one per conflicting record), '\n53\t    '\"source_seqs\": [<int seq>...], \"owner\": \"<for commitments, who>\"|null, '\n54\t    '\"due_iso\": \"<ISO date if a due date is stated>\"|null, \"confidence\": <0..1>}.'\n55\t)\n56\t\n57\t\n58\t# a fast, cheap model is ideal for this mechanical extraction task.\n59\tFAST_MODEL = \"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001\"\n60\t\n61\t\n62\t@runtime_checkable\n63\tclass ExtractionProvider(Protocol):\n64\t    def extract(self, records: list[SpineRecord]) -> list[CandidateFact]: ...\n65\t\n66\t\n67\tdef render_window(records: Iterable[SpineRecord], *, max_chars: int = 1200) -> str:\n68\t    lines = []\n69\t    for r in records:\n70\t        txt = (r.text() or \"\").replace(\"\\n\", \" \").strip()\n71\t        if not txt:\n72\t            continue\n73\t        lines.append(f\"[seq {r.seq}] ({r.kind} · {r.actor}) {txt[:max_chars]}\")\n74\t    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n75\t\n76\t\n77\tdef build_prompt(records: list[SpineRecord]) -> str:\n78\t    \"\"\"The extraction prompt every LLM provider sends (doctrine + schema + rendered window).\"\"\"\n79\t    return f\"{DOCTRINE}\\n{_SCHEMA_HINT}\\n\\nRECORDS:\\n{render_window(records)}\\n\"\n80\t\n81\t\n82\tdef parse_candidates(raw: str, *, extractor: str) -> list[CandidateFact]:\n83\t    \"\"\"Robustly pull the JSON array out of a model response (tolerates ```json fences / prose).\"\"\"\n84\t    if not raw:\n85\t        return []\n86\t    fenced = re.search(r\"```(?:json)?\\s*(\\[.*?\\])\\s*```\", raw, re.S)\n87\t    body = fenced.group(1) if fenced else None\n88\t    if body is None:\n89\t        start, end = raw.find(\"[\"), raw.rfind(\"]\")\n90\t        body = raw[start:end + 1] if 0 <= start < end else None\n91\t    if body is None:\n92\t        return []\n93\t    try:\n94\t        items = json.loads(body)\n95\t    except json.JSONDecodeError:\n96\t        return []\n97\t    out = []\n98\t    for it in items if isinstance(items, list) else []:\n99\t        if not isinstance(it, dict) or it.get(\"kind\") not in (\"decision\", \"commitment\", \"entity\", \"contradiction\"):\n100\t            continue\n101\t        seqs = it.get(\"source_seqs\") or []\n102\t        seqs = [int(s) for s in seqs if isinstance(s, (int, float, str)) and str(s).lstrip(\"-\").isdigit()]\n103\t        raw_quotes = it.get(\"quotes\") or []\n104\t        quotes = tuple(str(q) for q in raw_quotes if isinstance(q, str) and q.strip()) if isinstance(raw_quotes, list) else ()\n105\t        out.append(CandidateFact(\n106\t            kind=it[\"kind\"], subject=str(it.get(\"subject\", \"\")).strip(),\n107\t            statement=str(it.get(\"statement\", \"\")).strip(), quote=str(it.get(\"quote\", \"\")),\n108\t            source_seqs=seqs, model_confidence=float(it.get(\"confidence\", 0.5) or 0.5),\n109\t            owner=(it.get(\"owner\") or None), due_iso=(it.get(\"due_iso\") or None),\n110\t            extractor=extractor, quotes=quotes))\n111\t    return out\n112\t\n113\t\n114\tclass AgentProvider:\n115\t    \"\"\"`--provider claude`: headless Claude Code on the MAX plan (D5, owner's preference).\n116\t    Spawns `claude -p --model <fast> <prompt>` per window — no metered API key needed. A fast\n117\t    model (Haiku) keeps each batch quick and cheap for this mechanical extraction.\"\"\"\n118\t\n119\t    def __init__(self, claude_bin: str = \"/home/kali/.local/bin/claude\",\n120\t                 model: str = FAST_MODEL, timeout: int = 180) -> None:\n121\t        self.claude_bin = claude_bin\n122\t        self.model = model\n123\t        self.timeout = timeout\n124\t\n125\t    def extract(self, records: list[SpineRecord]) -> list[CandidateFact]:\n126\t        if not records:\n127\t            return []\n128\t        cmd = [self.claude_bin, \"-p\", build_prompt(records)]\n129\t        if self.model:\n130\t            cmd += [\"--model\", self.model]\n131\t        try:\n132\t            proc = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=self.timeout)\n133\t        except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError):\n134\t            return []\n135\t        return parse_candidates(proc.stdout, extractor=\"agent\")\n136\t\n137\t\n138\tclass ApiProvider:\n139\t    \"\"\"`--provider api`: the metered Anthropic Messages API (Claude API key). Reads the key\n140\t    from SIGIL_ANTHROPIC_API_KEY / ANTHROPIC_API_KEY (or ~/.sigil/sigil.env via config). Raw\n141\t    HTTP — no SDK dependency. Use when you want the API rather than the Max subscription.\"\"\"\n142\t\n143\t    def __init__(self, model: str = FAST_MODEL, api_key: str | None = None,\n144\t                 timeout: int = 120, max_tokens: int = 2048) -> None:\n145\t        import os\n146\t        self.model = model\n147\t        self.api_key = api_key or os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_ANTHROPIC_API_KEY\") or os.environ.get(\"ANTHROPIC_API_KEY\")\n148\t        self.timeout = timeout\n149\t        self.max_tokens = max_tokens\n150\t\n151\t    def extract(self, records: list[SpineRecord]) -> list[CandidateFact]:\n152\t        if not records:\n153\t            return []\n154\t        if not self.api_key:\n155\t            raise RuntimeError(\"ApiProvider needs an API key — set ANTHROPIC_API_KEY (or SIGIL_ANTHROPIC_API_KEY)\")\n156\t        import json as _json\n157\t        import urllib.request\n158\t        body = _json.dumps({\n159\t            \"model\": self.model, \"max_tokens\": self.max_tokens,\n160\t            \"messages\": [{\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": build_prompt(records)}],\n161\t        }).encode(\"utf-8\")\n162\t        req = urllib.request.Request(\n163\t            \"https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages\", data=body, method=\"POST\",\n164\t            headers={\"content-type\": \"application/json\", \"x-api-key\": self.api_key,\n165\t                     \"anthropic-version\": \"2023-06-01\"})\n166\t        try:\n167\t            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=self.timeout) as resp:\n168\t                payload = _json.loads(resp.read().decode(\"utf-8\"))\n169\t        except (urllib.error.URLError, OSError, ValueError):\n170\t            return []\n171\t        text = \"\".join(b.get(\"text\", \"\") for b in payload.get(\"content\", []) if isinstance(b, dict))\n172\t        return parse_candidates(text, extractor=\"api\")\n173\t\n174\t\n175\tclass LocalProvider:\n176\t    \"\"\"`--provider local`: a local Ollama model (fully offline, zero cost, no data leaves the\n177\t    box). Requires Ollama running at OLLAMA_HOST (default 127.0.0.1:11434) with `model` pulled.\"\"\"\n178\t\n179\t    def __init__(self, model: str = \"llama3.1\", host: str | None = None, timeout: int = 180) -> None:\n180\t        import os\n181\t        self.model = model\n182\t        self.host = host or os.environ.get(\"OLLAMA_HOST\") or \"http://127.0.0.1:11434\"\n183\t        self.timeout = timeout\n184\t\n185\t    def extract(self, records: list[SpineRecord]) -> list[CandidateFact]:\n186\t        if not records:\n187\t            return []\n188\t        import json as _json\n189\t        import urllib.request\n190\t        body = _json.dumps({\"model\": self.model, \"prompt\": build_prompt(records), \"stream\": False}).encode(\"utf-8\")\n191\t        req = urllib.request.Request(f\"{self.host.rstrip('/')}/api/generate\", data=body, method=\"POST\",\n192\t                                     headers={\"content-type\": \"application/json\"})\n193\t        try:\n194\t            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=self.timeout) as resp:\n195\t                payload = _json.loads(resp.read().decode(\"utf-8\"))\n196\t        except (urllib.error.URLError, OSError, ValueError):\n197\t            return []\n198\t        return parse_candidates(payload.get(\"response\", \"\"), extractor=\"local\")\n199\t\n200\t\n201\tclass ReplayProvider:\n202\t    \"\"\"Replays a captured fixture (a JSON array of candidate dicts). Zero cost; proves the\n203\t    parse/gate path end-to-end without a live call.\"\"\"\n204\t\n205\t    def __init__(self, fixture: str | Path) -> None:\n206\t        self.fixture = Path(fixture)\n207\t\n208\t    def extract(self, records: list[SpineRecord]) -> list[CandidateFact]:\n209\t        return parse_candidates(self.fixture.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"), extractor=\"replay\")\n210\t\n211\t\n212\t_DECISION_CUES = (\"decided to\", \"we'll go with\", \"going with\", \"chose to\", \"chosen:\", \"decision:\")\n213\t_COMMIT_CUES = (\"i'll \", \"i will \", \"we'll \", \"commit to\", \"todo:\", \"next step\", \"by end of\", \"due \")\n214\t\n215\t\n216\tclass HeuristicProvider:\n217\t    \"\"\"Offline, no-LLM keyword extractor — a weak fallback and a deterministic test double.\n218\t    Emits a candidate per cue-bearing sentence, quoting that sentence verbatim (so the gate\n219\t    grounds it). Deliberately conservative; the AgentProvider is the real extractor.\"\"\"\n220\t\n221\t    def extract(self, records: list[SpineRecord]) -> list[CandidateFact]:\n222\t        out = []\n223\t        for r in records:\n224\t            for sent in re.split(r\"(?<=[.!?])\\s+\", (r.text() or \"\")):\n225\t                low = sent.lower().strip()\n226\t                if not low:\n227\t                    continue\n228\t                if any(c in low for c in _DECISION_CUES):\n229\t                    out.append(CandidateFact(\"decision\", low[:60], sent.strip(), sent.strip(),\n230\t                                             [r.seq], 0.5, extractor=\"heuristic\"))\n231\t                elif any(c in low for c in _COMMIT_CUES):\n232\t                    out.append(CandidateFact(\"commitment\", low[:60], sent.strip(), sent.strip(),\n233\t                                             [r.seq], 0.5, owner=\"owner\", extractor=\"heuristic\"))\n234\t        return out\n235\t"}]},"uuid":"270ff7f4-f597-4b56-8107-4f5c4849666b","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:28.581Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"3c538f8c-13ac-488e-b844-b42a552e6985","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"ca115838-b532-4773-916c-161d40111b27","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a9a9282aa914a9b3f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01CEZLuVTiPxhraUTHghKVHX","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Real voice backends (SIGIL §8). Two tiers:\n2\t\n3\t  * ZERO-dep, always-available: `EnergyVad` (RMS), `EnergyWake` (speech-onset gate),\n4\t    `FileAudioSource`/`FileSink` (stdlib `wave` + numpy), `SilenceTts` (fallback). These let the\n5\t    whole pipeline run on a WAV file with no model downloads.\n6\t  * OPTIONAL ML (lazy-imported, graceful if absent): `SileroVad`, `OpenWakeWord`, `WhisperAsr`\n7\t    (faster-whisper), `PiperTts`, `MicAudioSource`, `SpeakerSink`.\n8\t\n9\tThe pipeline (`pipeline.VoicePipeline`) is agnostic to which tier is wired.\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\tfrom typing import Iterator, List\n13\t\n14\timport numpy as np\n15\t\n16\tfrom .components import FRAME_SAMPLES, SAMPLE_RATE\n17\t\n18\t\n19\t# ---- zero-dependency backends ---------------------------------------------------------------\n20\t\n21\tclass EnergyVad:\n22\t    \"\"\"RMS voice-activity detection over an int16 frame — no model. A reasonable default; Silero\n23\t    is the upgrade for noisy environments.\"\"\"\n24\t    def __init__(self, threshold: float = 500.0):\n25\t        self.threshold = threshold\n26\t\n27\t    def is_speech(self, frame) -> bool:\n28\t        x = np.asarray(frame, dtype=np.float32)\n29\t        if x.size == 0:\n30\t            return False\n31\t        return float(np.sqrt(np.mean(x * x))) >= self.threshold\n32\t\n33\t\n34\tclass EnergyWake:\n35\t    \"\"\"A trivial 'wake' that fires after `onset_frames` consecutive speech frames — a stand-in for\n36\t    the real custom-'SIGIL' openWakeWord model (which needs training data). Good for push-to-talk\n37\t    / always-listening use; swap in OpenWakeWord for hands-free wake.\"\"\"\n38\t    def __init__(self, vad: EnergyVad | None = None, onset_frames: int = 3):\n39\t        self.vad = vad or EnergyVad()\n40\t        self.onset_frames = onset_frames\n41\t        self._run = 0\n42\t\n43\t    def detect(self, frame) -> bool:\n44\t        if self.vad.is_speech(frame):\n45\t            self._run += 1\n46\t        else:\n47\t            self._run = 0\n48\t        if self._run >= self.onset_frames:\n49\t            self._run = 0\n50\t            return True\n51\t        return False\n52\t\n53\t    def reset(self) -> None:\n54\t        self._run = 0\n55\t\n56\t\n57\tdef _resample_i16(audio: np.ndarray, src: int, dst: int) -> np.ndarray:\n58\t    if src == dst or audio.size == 0:\n59\t        return audio\n60\t    n = int(round(len(audio) * dst / src))\n61\t    xp = np.linspace(0, 1, num=len(audio), endpoint=False)\n62\t    x = np.linspace(0, 1, num=n, endpoint=False)\n63\t    return np.interp(x, xp, audio.astype(np.float32)).astype(np.int16)\n64\t\n65\t\n66\tclass _FrameBuffer:\n67\t    \"\"\"Slice an arbitrary int16 stream into fixed-size frames, carrying the leftover across pushes\n68\t    (so no partial-frame audio is dropped between chunks); `flush` emits the final zero-padded frame.\"\"\"\n69\t    def __init__(self, frame_samples: int = FRAME_SAMPLES):\n70\t        self.n = frame_samples\n71\t        self._resid = np.zeros(0, dtype=np.int16)\n72\t\n73\t    def push(self, audio) -> Iterator[np.ndarray]:\n74\t        buf = np.concatenate([self._resid, np.asarray(audio, dtype=np.int16)])\n75\t        i = 0\n76\t        while i + self.n <= len(buf):\n77\t            yield buf[i:i + self.n]\n78\t            i += self.n\n79\t        self._resid = buf[i:].copy()\n80\t\n81\t    def flush(self) -> Iterator[np.ndarray]:\n82\t        if self._resid.size:\n83\t            frame = np.zeros(self.n, dtype=np.int16)\n84\t            frame[:self._resid.size] = self._resid\n85\t            self._resid = np.zeros(0, dtype=np.int16)\n86\t            yield frame\n87\t\n88\t\n89\tdef _decode_wav(raw: bytes, sampwidth: int) -> np.ndarray:\n90\t    if sampwidth == 2:\n91\t        return np.frombuffer(raw, dtype=np.int16)\n92\t    if sampwidth == 1:  # 8-bit unsigned PCM → int16\n93\t        return ((np.frombuffer(raw, dtype=np.uint8).astype(np.int32) - 128) * 256).astype(np.int16)\n94\t    raise ValueError(f\"unsupported WAV sample width {sampwidth * 8}-bit (need 8- or 16-bit PCM)\")\n95\t\n96\t\n97\tclass FileAudioSource:\n98\t    \"\"\"Yield 20-ms int16 mono frames from a WAV (stdlib `wave`), down-mixed + resampled to 16 kHz.\n99\t    Handles 8-/16-bit PCM, and emits the final (zero-padded) partial frame — no audio dropped.\"\"\"\n100\t    def __init__(self, path: str, frame_samples: int = FRAME_SAMPLES, rate: int = SAMPLE_RATE):\n101\t        self.path, self.frame_samples, self.rate = path, frame_samples, rate\n102\t\n103\t    def frames(self) -> Iterator[np.ndarray]:\n104\t        import wave\n105\t        with wave.open(self.path, \"rb\") as w:\n106\t            src_rate, ch, n, sw = w.getframerate(), w.getnchannels(), w.getnframes(), w.getsampwidth()\n107\t            raw = w.readframes(n)\n108\t        audio = _decode_wav(raw, sw)\n109\t        if ch > 1:\n110\t            audio = audio.reshape(-1, ch).mean(axis=1).astype(np.int16)\n111\t        audio = _resample_i16(audio, src_rate, self.rate)\n112\t        fb = _FrameBuffer(self.frame_samples)\n113\t        yield from fb.push(audio)\n114\t        yield from fb.flush()\n115\t\n116\t\n117\tclass FileSink:\n118\t    \"\"\"Collect played frames and write them to a WAV on close (16 kHz mono int16).\"\"\"\n119\t    def __init__(self, path: str, rate: int = SAMPLE_RATE):\n120\t        self.path, self.rate, self._buf = path, rate, []\n121\t\n122\t    def play(self, frame) -> None:\n123\t        arr = frame if isinstance(frame, np.ndarray) else np.zeros(FRAME_SAMPLES, dtype=np.int16)\n124\t        self._buf.append(np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.int16))\n125\t\n126\t    def close(self) -> None:\n127\t        import wave\n128\t        data = np.concatenate(self._buf) if self._buf else np.zeros(0, dtype=np.int16)\n129\t        with wave.open(self.path, \"wb\") as w:\n130\t            w.setnchannels(1); w.setsampwidth(2); w.setframerate(self.rate)\n131\t            w.writeframes(data.tobytes())\n132\t\n133\t\n134\tclass SilenceTts:\n135\t    \"\"\"Fallback TTS: emits ~`dur_per_char` of silence per character as 20-ms frames — so the\n136\t    full-duplex/barge-in pipeline runs (and its timing is realistic) without a real voice model.\"\"\"\n137\t    def __init__(self, ms_per_char: int = 60):\n138\t        self.ms_per_char = ms_per_char\n139\t\n140\t    def synth(self, text: str) -> Iterator[np.ndarray]:\n141\t        n_frames = max(1, (len(text) * self.ms_per_char) // 20)\n142\t        for _ in range(n_frames):\n143\t            yield np.zeros(FRAME_SAMPLES, dtype=np.int16)\n144\t\n145\t\n146\t# ---- optional ML backends (lazy imports) ----------------------------------------------------\n147\t\n148\tclass WhisperAsr:\n149\t    \"\"\"faster-whisper streaming ASR. `model` defaults to 'tiny' (fast; ~40 MB). Lazy-loaded.\"\"\"\n150\t    def __init__(self, model: str = \"tiny\", compute_type: str = \"int8\"):\n151\t        from faster_whisper import WhisperModel  # optional dep\n152\t        self._m = WhisperModel(model, device=\"cpu\", compute_type=compute_type)\n153\t\n154\t    def transcribe(self, frames: List) -> str:\n155\t        if not frames:\n156\t            return \"\"\n157\t        audio = np.concatenate([np.asarray(f, dtype=np.int16) for f in frames]).astype(np.float32) / 32768.0\n158\t        segments, _ = self._m.transcribe(audio, language=\"en\", vad_filter=False)\n159\t        return \" \".join(s.text.strip() for s in segments).strip()\n160\t\n161\t\n162\tclass SileroVad:\n163\t    def __init__(self, threshold: float = 0.5):\n164\t        import torch  # optional dep\n165\t        model, _ = torch.hub.load(\"snakers4/silero-vad\", \"silero_vad\", trust_repo=True)\n166\t        self._m, self._t, self._torch = model, threshold, torch\n167\t\n168\t    def is_speech(self, frame) -> bool:\n169\t        x = self._torch.from_numpy(np.asarray(frame, dtype=np.float32) / 32768.0)\n170\t        return float(self._m(x, SAMPLE_RATE).item()) >= self._t\n171\t\n172\t\n173\tclass OpenWakeWord:\n174\t    def __init__(self, model: str = \"hey_jarvis\", threshold: float = 0.5):\n175\t        from openwakeword.model import Model  # optional dep; custom 'SIGIL' model trains later\n176\t        self._m, self._t, self._name = Model(wakeword_models=[model]), threshold, model\n177\t\n178\t    def detect(self, frame) -> bool:\n179\t        scores = self._m.predict(np.asarray(frame, dtype=np.int16))\n180\t        return any(v >= self._t for v in scores.values())\n181\t\n182\t    def reset(self) -> None:\n183\t        try:\n184\t            self._m.reset()\n185\t        except Exception:\n186\t            pass\n187\t\n188\t\n189\tclass PiperTts:\n190\t    \"\"\"Piper local TTS. `voice` is a path to a .onnx voice model (downloaded separately).\"\"\"\n191\t    def __init__(self, voice: str):\n192\t        from piper.voice import PiperVoice  # optional dep\n193\t        self._v = PiperVoice.load(voice)\n194\t\n195\t    def synth(self, text: str) -> Iterator[np.ndarray]:\n196\t        fb = _FrameBuffer(FRAME_SAMPLES)\n197\t        for chunk in self._v.synthesize_stream_raw(text):\n198\t            yield from fb.push(np.frombuffer(chunk, dtype=np.int16))\n199\t        yield from fb.flush()\n200\t\n201\t\n202\tclass ElevenLabsTts:\n203\t    \"\"\"ElevenLabs cloud TTS (SIGIL owner's choice). Requests raw pcm_16000 so the pipeline can\n204\t    play/barge-in at the frame level. Needs ELEVENLABS_API_KEY (env or ~/.sigil/sigil.env). NOTE:\n205\t    a third-party service — the response TEXT leaves the machine; the local `PiperTts` is the\n206\t    sovereign alternative.\"\"\"\n207\t    def __init__(self, voice_id: str | None = None, model_id: str = \"eleven_flash_v2_5\",\n208\t                 api_key: str | None = None, timeout: int = 30):\n209\t        import os\n210\t        # the JARVIS voice_id (found via find_voices/`--find-voice jarvis`) is persisted as\n211\t        # SIGIL_TTS_VOICE_ID; fall back to the stock 'Rachel' only if nothing is configured.\n212\t        self.voice_id = voice_id or os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_TTS_VOICE_ID\") or \"21m00Tcm4TlvDq8ikWAM\"\n213\t        self.model_id, self.timeout = model_id, timeout\n214\t        self.api_key = api_key or os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_ELEVENLABS_API_KEY\") or os.environ.get(\"ELEVENLABS_API_KEY\")\n215\t\n216\t    def synth(self, text: str) -> Iterator[np.ndarray]:\n217\t        import json\n218\t        import urllib.request\n219\t        if not self.api_key:\n220\t            raise RuntimeError(\"ElevenLabsTts needs ELEVENLABS_API_KEY (env or ~/.sigil/sigil.env)\")\n221\t        url = (f\"https://api.elevenlabs.io/v1/text-to-speech/{self.voice_id}/stream\"\n222\t               f\"?output_format=pcm_16000\")\n223\t        body = json.dumps({\"text\": text, \"model_id\": self.model_id}).encode(\"utf-8\")\n224\t        req = urllib.request.Request(url, data=body, method=\"POST\",\n225\t                                     headers={\"xi-api-key\": self.api_key, \"content-type\": \"application/json\"})\n226\t        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=self.timeout) as resp:\n227\t            pcm = resp.read()\n228\t        fb = _FrameBuffer(FRAME_SAMPLES)\n229\t        yield from fb.push(np.frombuffer(pcm, dtype=np.int16))\n230\t        yield from fb.flush()\n231\t\n232\t\n233\tclass ElevenLabsAsr:\n234\t    \"\"\"ElevenLabs 'Scribe' cloud STT (SIGIL owner's choice). Sends the captured utterance as a WAV\n235\t    to /v1/speech-to-text. Needs ELEVENLABS_API_KEY. NOTE: a third-party service — the AUDIO leaves\n236\t    the machine; the local `WhisperAsr` is the sovereign alternative.\"\"\"\n237\t    def __init__(self, model_id: str = \"scribe_v1\", api_key: str | None = None, timeout: int = 30):\n238\t        import os\n239\t        self.model_id, self.timeout = model_id, timeout\n240\t        self.api_key = api_key or os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_ELEVENLABS_API_KEY\") or os.environ.get(\"ELEVENLABS_API_KEY\")\n241\t\n242\t    def transcribe(self, frames: List) -> str:\n243\t        import io\n244\t        import json\n245\t        import urllib.request\n246\t        import uuid\n247\t        import wave\n248\t        if not self.api_key:\n249\t            raise RuntimeError(\"ElevenLabsAsr needs ELEVENLABS_API_KEY (env or ~/.sigil/sigil.env)\")\n250\t        if not frames:\n251\t            return \"\"\n252\t        # assemble the frames into an in-memory 16 kHz mono WAV\n253\t        audio = np.concatenate([np.asarray(f, dtype=np.int16) for f in frames])\n254\t        buf = io.BytesIO()\n255\t        with wave.open(buf, \"wb\") as w:\n256\t            w.setnchannels(1); w.setsampwidth(2); w.setframerate(SAMPLE_RATE); w.writeframes(audio.tobytes())\n257\t        wav_bytes = buf.getvalue()\n258\t        # minimal multipart/form-data (model_id + file)\n259\t        boundary = f\"----sigil{uuid.uuid4().hex}\"\n260\t        parts = [\n261\t            f\"--{boundary}\\r\\nContent-Disposition: form-data; boundary={boundary}\"})\n269\t        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=self.timeout) as resp:\n270\t            payload = json.loads(resp.read().decode(\"utf-8\"))\n271\t        return str(payload.get(\"text\", \"\")).strip()\n272\t\n273\t\n274\tdef find_voices(query: str = \"jarvis\", api_key: str | None = None, timeout: int = 30) -> List[dict]:\n275\t    \"\"\"Search the ElevenLabs SHARED voice library for `query` (e.g. 'jarvis' → the Iron-Man-style\n276\t    British butler voice). Returns [{name, voice_id, accent, category, description}] so the owner\n277\t    can pick + pin one via `set_voice`.\"\"\"\n278\t    import json\n279\t    import os\n280\t    import urllib.parse\n281\t    import urllib.request\n282\t    key = api_key or os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_ELEVENLABS_API_KEY\") or os.environ.get(\"ELEVENLABS_API_KEY\")\n283\t    if not key:\n284\t        raise RuntimeError(\"find_voices needs ELEVENLABS_API_KEY (env or ~/.sigil/sigil.env)\")\n285\t    url = \"https://api.elevenlabs.io/v1/shared-voices?\" + urllib.parse.urlencode({\"search\": query, \"page_size\": 25})\n286\t    req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={\"xi-api-key\": key})\n287\t    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as resp:\n288\t        payload = json.loads(resp.read().decode(\"utf-8\"))\n289\t    out = []\n290\t    for v in payload.get(\"voices\", []):\n291\t        langs = v.get(\"verified_languages\") or []\n292\t        out.append({\n293\t            \"name\": v.get(\"name\"),\n294\t            \"voice_id\": v.get(\"voice_id\"),\n295\t            \"accent\": v.get(\"accent\") or (langs[0].get(\"accent\", \"\") if langs else \"\"),\n296\t            \"category\": v.get(\"category\"),\n297\t            \"description\": (v.get(\"description\") or \"\")[:90],\n298\t        })\n299\t    return out\n300\t\n301\t\n302\tdef set_voice(voice_id: str) -> None:\n303\t    \"\"\"Persist the chosen TTS voice_id as SIGIL_TTS_VOICE_ID in ~/.sigil/sigil.env (loaded by\n304\t    config at import), so ElevenLabsTts uses it by default everywhere.\"\"\"\n305\t    from ..config import SIGIL_HOME\n306\t    env_path = SIGIL_HOME / \"sigil.env\"\n307\t    lines = []\n308\t    if env_path.exists():\n309\t        lines = [ln for ln in env_path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").splitlines()\n310\t                 if not ln.strip().startswith(\"SIGIL_TTS_VOICE_ID=\")]\n311\t    lines.append(f\"SIGIL_TTS_VOICE_ID={voice_id}\")\n312\t    env_path.write_text(\"\\n\".join(lines) + \"\\n\", encoding=\"utf-8\")\n313\t\n314\t\n315\tclass MicAudioSource:\n316\t    def __init__(self, frame_samples: int = FRAME_SAMPLES, rate: int = SAMPLE_RATE):\n317\t        self.frame_samples, self.rate = frame_samples, rate\n318\t\n319\t    def frames(self) -> Iterator[np.ndarray]:\n320\t        import sounddevice as sd  # optional dep\n321\t        with sd.InputStream(samplerate=self.rate, channels=1, dtype=\"int16\", blocksize=self.frame_samples) as stream:\n322\t            while True:\n323\t                data, _ = stream.read(self.frame_samples)\n324\t                yield data[:, 0].copy()\n325\t\n326\t\n327\tclass SpeakerSink:\n328\t    def __init__(self, rate: int = SAMPLE_RATE):\n329\t        import sounddevice as sd  # optional dep\n330\t        self._stream = sd.OutputStream(samplerate=rate, channels=1, dtype=\"int16\")\n331\t        self._stream.start()\n332\t\n333\t    def play(self, frame) -> None:\n334\t        self._stream.write(np.asarray(frame, dtype=np.int16))\n335\t\n336\t    def close(self) -> None:\n337\t        try:\n338\t            self._stream.stop(); self._stream.close()\n339\t        except Exception:\n340\t            pass\n341\t"}]},"uuid":"0ae4fadb-2871-4bc0-81dc-7a96041116bb","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:35.528Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"ca115838-b532-4773-916c-161d40111b27","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

needs a mic + speaker + optional ML).\"\"\"\n3\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n4\t\n5\tfrom .pipeline import VoicePipeline\n6\t\n7\t\n8\tclass _StubAsr:\n9\t    \"\"\"Used when faster-whisper is not installed — the pipeline still runs end-to-end (segmenting\n10\t    real audio, dispatching, speaking); only the transcript is a placeholder.\"\"\"\n11\t    def transcribe(self, frames):\n12\t        return \"(no ASR model installed — run: pip install faster-whisper)\"\n13\t\n14\t\n15\tdef _make_asr(kind: str):\n16\t    if kind == \"stub\":\n17\t        return _StubAsr()\n18\t    if kind == \"elevenlabs\":\n19\t        from .backends import ElevenLabsAsr\n20\t        a = ElevenLabsAsr()\n21\t        if not a.api_key:\n22\t            print(\"  [asr] ElevenLabs key not set (ELEVENLABS_API_KEY); using stub\")\n23\t            return _StubAsr()\n24\t        return a\n25\t    try:\n26\t        from .backends import WhisperAsr\n27\t        return WhisperAsr(model=\"tiny\")\n28\t    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001\n29\t        print(f\"  [asr] faster-whisper unavailable ({e}); using stub\")\n30\t        return _StubAsr()\n31\t\n32\t\n33\tdef _make_tts(kind: str, voice: str | None):\n34\t    from .backends import SilenceTts\n35\t    if kind == \"elevenlabs\":\n36\t        from .backends import ElevenLabsTts\n37\t        t = ElevenLabsTts(voice_id=voice)   # JARVIS voice via SIGIL_TTS_VOICE_ID / --tts-voice\n38\t        if not t.api_key:\n39\t            print(\"  [tts] ElevenLabs key not set (ELEVENLABS_API_KEY); using silence fallback\")\n40\t            return SilenceTts()\n41\t        return t\n42\t    if kind == \"piper\" and voice:\n43\t        from .backends import PiperTts\n44\t        return PiperTts(voice)\n45\t    return SilenceTts()\n46\t\n47\t\n48\tdef run_file(wav_in: str, wav_out: str, *, asr: str = \"auto\", tts: str = \"silence\",\n49\t             tts_voice: str | None = None) -> VoicePipeline:\n50\t    \"\"\"Run the full pipeline over a WAV file (EnergyVad + EnergyWake + ASR + KERNEL + TTS → WAV).\n51\t    Returns the pipeline so the caller can read transcript / response / events.\"\"\"\n52\t    from .backends import EnergyVad, EnergyWake, FileAudioSource, FileSink\n53\t    from .dispatch import KernelDispatch\n54\t\n55\t    vad = EnergyVad()\n56\t    sink = FileSink(wav_out)\n57\t    p = VoicePipeline(vad, EnergyWake(vad), _make_asr(asr), _make_tts(tts, tts_voice), sink, KernelDispatch())\n58\t    p.run(FileAudioSource(wav_in).frames())\n59\t    sink.close()\n60\t    return p\n61\t\n62\t\n63\tdef run_mic(*, asr: str = \"elevenlabs\", wake: str = \"energy\", tts: str = \"elevenlabs\",\n64\t            tts_voice: str | None = None) -> None:\n65\t    \"\"\"Live full-duplex loop (needs a working mic + speaker). ASR/TTS default to ElevenLabs (the\n66\t    owner's choice); wake=openWakeWord if `wake=oww`, else the energy-onset stand-in.\"\"\"\n67\t    from .backends import EnergyVad, EnergyWake, MicAudioSource, OpenWakeWord, SpeakerSink\n68\t    from .dispatch import KernelDispatch\n69\t\n70\t    vad = EnergyVad()\n71\t    wake_c = OpenWakeWord() if wake == \"oww\" else EnergyWake(vad)\n72\t    sink = SpeakerSink()\n73\t    p = VoicePipeline(vad, wake_c, _make_asr(asr), _make_tts(tts, tts_voice), sink, KernelDispatch())\n74\t    print(\"SIGIL voice: listening (Ctrl-C to stop) …\")\n75\t    try:\n76\t        p.run(MicAudioSource().frames())\n77\t    except KeyboardInterrupt:\n78\t        pass\n79\t    finally:\n80\t        sink.close()\n81\t"}]},"uuid":"0667b4d3-fcfe-4241-bb98-4358db07bf8a","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:36.043Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"29e33ce1-c18d-4c0d-a75e-701f13fb5dd3","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
