{"parentUuid":"7cd21401-e514-4d0d-91d8-815a4c25e4a3","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a9a9282aa914a9b3f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01UwYffsNp5yzK9CwxoNp9k4","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The SIGIL voice pipeline (SIGIL §8) — a frame-driven, full-duplex state machine.\n2\t\n3\t    IDLE ──wake word──▶ LISTENING ──end-of-speech──▶ THINKING ──▶ SPEAKING ──done──▶ IDLE\n4\t      ▲                    │                                          │\n5\t      └────timeout─────────┘                       barge-in (VAD)─────┘  (cancel TTS, re-LISTEN)\n6\t\n7\tFull-duplex + barge-in are the acceptance bar (§8): while SPEAKING, every input frame is\n8\tVAD-checked, and `barge_in_frames` CONSECUTIVE speech frames (hysteresis) cancel the TTS and\n9\tre-enter LISTENING. The machine is driven one input frame at a time and holds no audio/ML deps\n10\titself (those are the injected components), so the whole behaviour is deterministically testable.\n11\t\n12\tLIVE-PATH NOTE: on a real mic+speaker the assistant's own TTS is captured by the mic, so\n13\tbarge-in needs acoustic-echo cancellation (AEC) or output ducking; without it, run the live\n14\tloop half-duplex (gate the mic while SPEAKING). Hysteresis alone does not defeat sustained\n15\tself-echo — AEC is the real fix and is a runtime/hardware concern, not a state-machine one.\"\"\"\n16\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n17\t\n18\tfrom enum import Enum\n19\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n20\t\n21\tfrom .components import Asr, AudioSink, Dispatch, Tts, Vad, WakeWord\n22\t\n23\t\n24\tclass State(Enum):\n25\t    IDLE = \"idle\"\n26\t    LISTENING = \"listening\"\n27\t    THINKING = \"thinking\"\n28\t    SPEAKING = \"speaking\"\n29\t\n30\t\n31\tclass VoicePipeline:\n32\t    def __init__(self, vad: Vad, wake: WakeWord, asr: Asr, tts: Tts, sink: AudioSink, dispatch: Dispatch,\n33\t                 *, silence_frames: int = 25, min_speech_frames: int = 3, listen_timeout_frames: int = 150,\n34\t                 max_utterance_frames: int = 1000, barge_in_frames: int = 4):\n35\t        self.vad, self.wake, self.asr, self.tts, self.sink, self.dispatch = vad, wake, asr, tts, sink, dispatch\n36\t        self.silence_frames = silence_frames          # ~500 ms of trailing silence ends the utterance\n37\t        self.min_speech_frames = min_speech_frames     # require real speech before ending\n38\t        self.listen_timeout_frames = listen_timeout_frames  # give up if no speech after wake (~3 s)\n39\t        self.max_utterance_frames = max_utterance_frames    # absolute cap so LISTENING always terminates (~20 s)\n40\t        self.barge_in_frames = barge_in_frames         # consecutive speech frames to CONFIRM a barge-in (hysteresis)\n41\t\n42\t        self.state = State.IDLE\n43\t        self.transcript: Optional[str] = None\n44\t        self.response: Optional[str] = None\n45\t        self.events: List[str] = []\n46\t        self._captured: List = []\n47\t        self._silence = 0\n48\t        self._speech = 0\n49\t        self._listen = 0\n50\t        self._tts_iter = None\n51\t        self._barge = 0\n52\t        self._barge_buf: List = []\n53\t\n54\t    # --- one input frame drives the machine --------------------------------------------------\n55\t    def on_frame(self, frame) -> None:\n56\t        if self.state is State.IDLE:\n57\t            if self.wake.detect(frame):\n58\t                self._to_listening([])\n59\t        elif self.state is State.LISTENING:\n60\t            self._listening(frame)\n61\t        elif self.state is State.SPEAKING:\n62\t            self._speaking(frame)\n63\t        # THINKING is transient (handled synchronously on entry); it never awaits input frames.\n64\t\n65\t    def _listening(self, frame) -> None:\n66\t        self._captured.append(frame)\n67\t        self._listen += 1\n68\t        if self.vad.is_speech(frame):\n69\t            self._speech += 1\n70\t            self._silence = 0\n71\t        else:\n72\t            self._silence += 1\n73\t        if self._speech >= self.min_speech_frames and self._silence >= self.silence_frames:\n74\t            self._to_thinking()\n75\t        elif self._listen >= self.max_utterance_frames:\n76\t            # absolute cap — LISTENING can never wedge (e.g. VAD stuck-high on ambient noise)\n77\t            if self._speech >= self.min_speech_frames:\n78\t                self.events.append(\"listening→thinking (max utterance)\")\n79\t                self._to_thinking()\n80\t            else:\n81\t                self.events.append(\"listening→idle (max, no speech)\")\n82\t                self._to_idle()\n83\t        elif self._speech < self.min_speech_frames and self._listen >= self.listen_timeout_frames:\n84\t            self.events.append(\"listening→idle (timeout, no speech)\")\n85\t            self._to_idle()\n86\t\n87\t    def _speaking(self, frame) -> None:\n88\t        # full-duplex barge-in with HYSTERESIS: only `barge_in_frames` CONSECUTIVE speech frames\n89\t        # count as a real interruption, so a single cough/click/TTS-echo blip can't self-abort the\n90\t        # answer. During the short confirm window the TTS keeps playing; on a silence frame the\n91\t        # counter resets. (A real mic+speaker still needs AEC or ducking — see the module note.)\n92\t        if self.vad.is_speech(frame):\n93\t            self._barge += 1\n94\t            self._barge_buf.append(frame)\n95\t            if self._barge >= self.barge_in_frames:\n96\t                self.events.append(\"barge-in: speaking→listening\")\n97\t                self._tts_iter = None\n98\t                self._to_listening(self._barge_buf, speech=self._barge)\n99\t            else:\n100\t                self._advance_tts()\n101\t        else:\n102\t            self._barge = 0\n103\t            self._barge_buf = []\n104\t            self._advance_tts()\n105\t\n106\t    def _advance_tts(self) -> None:\n107\t        try:\n108\t            self.sink.play(next(self._tts_iter))\n109\t        except StopIteration:\n110\t            self.events.append(\"speaking→idle\")\n111\t            self._to_idle()\n112\t\n113\t    # --- transitions -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n114\t    def _to_listening(self, captured: List, speech: int = 0) -> None:\n115\t        if self.state is State.IDLE:\n116\t            self.events.append(\"wake→listening\")\n117\t        self.state = State.LISTENING\n118\t        self._captured = list(captured)\n119\t        self._silence = 0\n120\t        self._speech = speech\n121\t        self._listen = len(captured)\n122\t\n123\t    def _to_thinking(self) -> None:\n124\t        self.events.append(\"listening→thinking\")\n125\t        self.state = State.THINKING\n126\t        # synchronous v1: transcribe → dispatch to the KERNEL → speak the response.\n127\t        self.transcript = self.asr.transcribe(self._captured)\n128\t        self.response = self.dispatch.send(self.transcript)\n129\t        self._to_speaking(self.response)\n130\t\n131\t    def _to_speaking(self, text: str) -> None:\n132\t        self.events.append(\"thinking→speaking\")\n133\t        self.state = State.SPEAKING\n134\t        self._tts_iter = iter(self.tts.synth(text))\n135\t        self._barge = 0\n136\t        self._barge_buf = []\n137\t\n138\t    def _to_idle(self) -> None:\n139\t        self.state = State.IDLE\n140\t        self._captured = []\n141\t        self._tts_iter = None\n142\t        self.wake.reset()\n143\t\n144\t    # --- driver: pump an audio source through the machine ------------------------------------\n145\t    def run(self, frames) -> None:\n146\t        \"\"\"Drive the pipeline from any iterable of input frames (a mic stream or a file). After a\n147\t        FINITE stream ends (file mode), drain any in-flight TTS so the whole answer is emitted\n148\t        (live/infinite streams play the TTS in real time, one chunk per input frame).\"\"\"\n149\t        for frame in frames:\n150\t            self.on_frame(frame)\n151\t        self.drain()\n152\t\n153\t    def drain(self) -> None:\n154\t        while self.state is State.SPEAKING and self._tts_iter is not None:\n155\t            self._advance_tts()\n156\t"}]},"uuid":"47c749ad-50b2-4718-8fe6-05b503d230da","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:36.530Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"7cd21401-e514-4d0d-91d8-815a4c25e4a3","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"b6ab3dac-dec8-4d52-98df-f0908d49e7a9","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a9a9282aa914a9b3f","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011Cd9rNGMjD7kVBafh8q9eV","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01AE8woUsHMW77vLwqmiuK3D","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/voice/dispatch.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":12539,"cache_read_input_tokens":33993,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":12539,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011Cd9rNFQRkHMzYQUqGayDX","attributionAgent":"Explore","type":"assistant","uuid":"271c76c2-c8ad-4e99-9c7b-7d8ca347eac8","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:47.238Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"271c76c2-c8ad-4e99-9c7b-7d8ca347eac8","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a9a9282aa914a9b3f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01AE8woUsHMW77vLwqmiuK3D","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Voice → KERNEL dispatch. Recognized text is routed through the Phase-1 KERNEL (`sigil-kernel\n2\task`), so a voice request crosses the SAME T0 router + WARDEN gate + signed action log as any\n3\tother — voice is just another interface onto the one authorized path. The spoken response is the\n4\tKERNEL's answer body (the bracketed [T0…]/[WARDEN…] status lines are stripped).\"\"\"\n5\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n6\t\n7\timport subprocess\n8\tfrom pathlib import Path\n9\t\n10\t\n11\tdef _default_bin() -> str:\n12\t    for p in (\"kernel/target/release/sigil-kernel\", \"kernel/target/debug/sigil-kernel\"):\n13\t        f = Path(\"/home/kali/sigil\") / p\n14\t        if f.exists():\n15\t            return str(f)\n16\t    return \"sigil-kernel\"\n17\t\n18\t\n19\tclass KernelDispatch:\n20\t    def __init__(self, kernel_bin: str | None = None, timeout: int = 60):\n21\t        self.kernel_bin = kernel_bin or _default_bin()\n22\t        self.timeout = timeout\n23\t\n24\t    # exact KERNEL status-line prefixes to strip (so a legitimate answer line that merely starts\n25\t    # with '[' is NOT dropped, and a BLOCKED/error is NOT masked as an answer).\n26\t    _STATUS = (\"[t0\", \"[warden\", \"[blocked\", \"[direct\", \"[dispatch\")\n27\t\n28\t    def send(self, text: str) -> str:\n29\t        text = (text or \"\").strip()\n30\t        if not text:\n31\t            return \"I didn't catch that.\"\n32\t        try:\n33\t            proc = subprocess.run([self.kernel_bin, \"ask\", text],\n34\t                                  capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=self.timeout)\n35\t        except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError) as e:\n36\t            return f\"the kernel is unavailable ({e})\"\n37\t        if proc.returncode != 0:\n38\t            tail = (proc.stderr or proc.stdout).strip().splitlines()\n39\t            return f\"that request was refused or errored: {tail[-1][:200]}\" if tail else \"that request was refused.\"\n40\t        if any(ln.lstrip().lower().startswith(\"[blocked\") for ln in proc.stdout.splitlines()):\n41\t            return \"that action needs your approval — it wasn't run.\"\n42\t        body = [ln.strip() for ln in proc.stdout.splitlines()\n43\t                if ln.strip() and not any(ln.lstrip().lower().startswith(s) for s in self._STATUS)]\n44\t        answer = \" \".join(body).strip()\n45\t        return answer[:600] if answer else \"there's no answer for that in memory yet.\"\n46\t"}]},"uuid":"ac5c6d34-2c7e-4b1f-82db-fb5636274d08","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:47.284Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"271c76c2-c8ad-4e99-9c7b-7d8ca347eac8","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

A `Frame` is a chunk\n5\tof mono 16-kHz audio (numpy int16/float32); the pipeline is frame-driven so full-duplex + barge-in\n6\tare testable without any audio hardware.\"\"\"\n7\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n8\t\n9\tfrom typing import Iterator, List, Optional, Protocol, runtime_checkable\n10\t\n11\ttry:\n12\t    import numpy as np\n13\t    Frame = \"np.ndarray\"\n14\texcept Exception:  # numpy is present in the venv, but keep the import soft\n15\t    np = None  # type: ignore\n16\t    Frame = object  # type: ignore\n17\t\n18\tSAMPLE_RATE = 16000\n19\tFRAME_MS = 20\n20\tFRAME_SAMPLES = SAMPLE_RATE * FRAME_MS // 1000  # 320 samples @ 16 kHz\n21\t\n22\t\n23\t@runtime_checkable\n24\tclass Vad(Protocol):\n25\t    \"\"\"Voice-activity detection over a single frame.\"\"\"\n26\t    def is_speech(self, frame) -> bool: ...\n27\t\n28\t\n29\t@runtime_checkable\n30\tclass WakeWord(Protocol):\n31\t    \"\"\"Streaming wake-word detector — returns True on the frame that completes the wake phrase.\"\"\"\n32\t    def detect(self, frame) -> bool: ...\n33\t    def reset(self) -> None: ...\n34\t\n35\t\n36\t@runtime_checkable\n37\tclass Asr(Protocol):\n38\t    \"\"\"Transcribe a captured utterance (a list of frames) to text.\"\"\"\n39\t    def transcribe(self, frames: List) -> str: ...\n40\t\n41\t\n42\t@runtime_checkable\n43\tclass Tts(Protocol):\n44\t    \"\"\"Synthesize speech as a stream of audio chunks the pipeline plays incrementally (so it can\n45\t    be CANCELLED mid-utterance for barge-in).\"\"\"\n46\t    def synth(self, text: str) -> Iterator: ...\n47\t\n48\t\n49\t@runtime_checkable\n50\tclass AudioSink(Protocol):\n51\t    \"\"\"Where synthesized audio goes (speaker, or a file/buffer in tests).\"\"\"\n52\t    def play(self, frame) -> None: ...\n53\t\n54\t\n55\t@runtime_checkable\n56\tclass Dispatch(Protocol):\n57\t    \"\"\"Send recognized text to the KERNEL and return the spoken response.\"\"\"\n58\t    def send(self, text: str) -> str: ...\n59\t\n60\t\n61\t# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n62\t# Deterministic mocks — drive the state machine in tests with zero audio/ML dependency.\n63\t# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n64\t\n65\tclass ScriptedVad:\n66\t    \"\"\"is_speech follows a per-frame script (list of bools), then repeats the last value.\"\"\"\n67\t    def __init__(self, script: List[bool]):\n68\t        self.script = script\n69\t        self.i = 0\n70\t\n71\t    def is_speech(self, frame) -> bool:\n72\t        v = self.script[min(self.i, len(self.script) - 1)] if self.script else False\n73\t        self.i += 1\n74\t        return v\n75\t\n76\t\n77\tclass ScriptedWake:\n78\t    \"\"\"detect fires True exactly ONCE ever, on frame index `fire_at` (latched — a real wake word\n79\t    fires per-utterance, so `reset` re-arms the frame counter but does not re-trigger a past hit).\"\"\"\n80\t    def __init__(self, fire_at: int):\n81\t        self.fire_at = fire_at\n82\t        self.i = 0\n83\t        self.fired = False\n84\t\n85\t    def detect(self, frame) -> bool:\n86\t        hit = self.i == self.fire_at and not self.fired\n87\t        if hit:\n88\t            self.fired = True\n89\t        self.i += 1\n90\t        return hit\n91\t\n92\t    def reset(self) -> None:\n93\t        self.i = 0\n94\t\n95\t\n96\tclass FixedAsr:\n97\t    def __init__(self, text: str):\n98\t        self.text = text\n99\t        self.calls: List[int] = []\n100\t\n101\t    def transcribe(self, frames: List) -> str:\n102\t        self.calls.append(len(frames))\n103\t        return self.text\n104\t\n105\t\n106\tclass ChunkTts:\n107\t    \"\"\"Yields `chunks` opaque audio chunks; records whether it was fully consumed (not cancelled).\"\"\"\n108\t    def __init__(self, chunks: int = 5):\n109\t        self.chunks = chunks\n110\t        self.spoken: List[str] = []\n111\t range(self.chunks):\n117\t            yield (\"chunk\", text, k)\n118\t        self.completed = True\n119\t\n120\t\n121\tclass BufferSink:\n122\t    def __init__(self):\n123\t        self.frames: List = []\n124\t\n125\t    def play(self, frame) -> None:\n126\t        self.frames.append(frame)\n127\t\n128\t\n129\tclass EchoDispatch:\n130\t    def __init__(self, response: str = \"done\"):\n131\t {"parentUuid":"51e2b622-758e-4099-9868-413fd49f7d3d","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a9a9282aa914a9b3f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01MgXXgaX3eoJfuYAN8LdaLi","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t//! SIGIL KERNEL — the palette (Phase 1). A command interface to the KERNEL: route an intent\n2\t//! through the cognition cascade (T0 → answer/escalate/dispatch), gate every tool invocation\n3\t//! through WARDEN (tier A0–A3, fail-closed), and append a signed, chained action-log record. The\n4\t//! self-audit replays that log verbatim (\"what did you do and why\", C18). A global hotkey +\n5\t//! voice daemon are Phase 1.5/2; this CLI meets the acceptance bar: a full task round-trip,\n6\t//! logged & tiered.\n7\t\n8\tuse std::path::PathBuf;\n9\tuse std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};\n10\t\n11\tuse clap::{Parser, Subcommand};\n12\tuse sigil_kernel::crypto::sha256_hex;\n13\tuse sigil_kernel::router::{route, Route};\n14\tuse sigil_kernel::tiers::Tier;\n15\tuse sigil_kernel::warden::{Verdict, Warden};\n16\tuse sigil_kernel::{anchor, cascade, memory};\n17\t\n18\t#[derive(Parser)]\n19\t#[command(name = \"sigil-kernel\", about = \"SIGIL KERNEL — routed, gated, logged intent\")]\n20\tstruct Cli {\n21\t    #[command(subcommand)]\n22\t    cmd: Cmd,\n23\t}\n24\t\n25\t#[derive(Subcommand)]\n26\tenum Cmd {\n27\t    /// Route an intent through the cascade (T0 classify → dispatch), gated + logged by WARDEN.\n28\t    Ask {\n29\t        #[arg(required = true, trailing_var_arg = true)]\n30\t        intent: Vec<String>,\n31\t    },\n32\t    /// Invoke a named tool directly through WARDEN — demonstrates tier classification + gating.\n33\t    Do {\n34\t        tool: String,\n35\t        #[arg(trailing_var_arg = true)]\n36\t        args: Vec<String>,\n37\t    },\n38\t    /// Replay the signed action log verbatim (self-audit, C18).\n39\t    Audit,\n40\t    /// Verify the action log's chain + signatures + the spine-anchored anti-rollback high-water.\n41\t    /// FAILS CLOSED if the anchor is unreachable/unestablished; pass --allow-unanchored for a\n42\t    /// genuine first run / offline check (weaker: local integrity only).\n43\t    Verify {\n44\t        #[arg(long)]\n45\t        allow_unanchored: bool,\n46\t    },\n47\t    /// Cross-anchor the current action-log head into the append-only Phase-0 spine (anti-rollback).\n48\t    Checkpoint,\n49\t    /// Print the WARDEN public key.\n50\t    Pubkey,\n51\t}\n52\t\n53\tfn warden_dir() -> PathBuf {\n54\t    let home = std::env::var(\"SIGIL_HOME\")\n55\t        .unwrap_or_else(|_| format!(\"{}/.sigil\", std::env::var(\"HOME\").unwrap_or_else(|_| \".\".into())));\n56\t    PathBuf::from(home).join(\"warden\")\n57\t}\n58\t\n59\tfn now() -> u64 {\n60\t    SystemTime::now().duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).map(|d| d.as_secs()).unwrap_or(0)\n61\t}\n62\t\n63\tfn args_hash(s: &str) -> String {\n64\t    format!(\"b3:{}\", &sha256_hex(s.as_bytes())[..12])\n65\t}\n66\t\n67\tfn main() {\n68\t    let cli = Cli::parse();\n69\t    let w = Warden::open(&warden_dir()).unwrap_or_else(|e| {\n70\t        eprintln!(\"cannot open WARDEN: {e}\");\n71\t        std::process::exit(1);\n72\t    });\n73\t\n74\t    match cli.cmd {\n75\t        Cmd::Ask { intent } => cmd_ask(&w, &intent.join(\" \")),\n76\t        Cmd::Do { tool, args } => cmd_do(&w, &tool, &args.join(\" \")),\n77\t        Cmd::Audit => cmd_audit(&w),\n78\t        Cmd::Verify { allow_unanchored } => cmd_verify(&w, allow_unanchored),\n79\t        Cmd::Checkpoint => cmd_checkpoint(&w),\n80\t        Cmd::Pubkey => println!(\"{}\", w.public_hex()),\n81\t    }\n82\t}\n83\t\n84\tfn anchor_set(w: &Warden, count: u64, head_hash: &str) -> std::io::Result<()> {\n85\t    let pubkey = w.public_hex();\n86\t    let sig = w.sign_hex(anchor::anchor_msg(count, head_hash, &pubkey).as_bytes());\n87\t    anchor::set(count, head_hash, &pubkey, &sig)\n88\t}\n89\t\n90\t/// Anchor the current head into the spine — best-effort, never fatal to the hot path.\n91\tfn anchor_head(w: &Warden) {\n92\t    if let Ok(Some((count, head_hash))) = w.head() {\n93\t        if count > 0 {\n94\t            let _ = anchor_set(w, count, &head_hash);\n95\t        }\n96\t    }\n97\t}\n98\t\n99\tfn cmd_checkpoint(w: &Warden) {\n100\t    match w.head() {\n101\t        Ok(Some((count, head_hash))) => match anchor_set(w, count, &head_hash) {\n102\t            Ok(()) => println!(\"anchored action-log head into the spine: count={count} head={}\", &head_hash[..16.min(head_hash.len())]),\n103\t            Err(e) => eprintln!(\"anchor failed: {e}\"),\n104\t        },\n105\t        Ok(None) => println!(\"nothing to anchor (action log empty)\"),\n106\t        Err(e) => eprintln!(\"cannot read head: {e}\"),\n107\t    }\n108\t}\n109\t\n110\tfn cmd_ask(w: &Warden, intent: &str) {\n111\t    let r = route(intent);\n112\t    // Route → the tool the KERNEL will invoke. Cognition (answer/plan) is A0-class (no external\n113\t    // effect); a real agent dispatch is recorded as an A1 intent note (the mesh is Phase 3).\n114\t    let tool = match r {\n115\t        Route::AnswerLocal => \"memory.search\",\n116\t        Route::EscalateT1 => \"cascade.answer.t1\",\n117\t        Route::EscalateT2 => \"cascade.answer.t2\",\n118\t        Route::DispatchAgent => \"agent.dispatch.note\",\n119\t    };\n120\t    let v = w.decide(tool);\n121\t    println!(\"[T0 {} → {}]  WARDEN: {} {}\", r.as_str(), tool, v.tier, v.decision.as_str());\n122\t\n123\t    if !v.decision.may_run() {\n124\t        block(w, \"KERNEL\", tool, intent, &v);\n125\t        return;\n126\t    }\n127\t\n128\t    let output = match r {\n129\t        Route::AnswerLocal => memory::search(intent, 5).unwrap_or_else(|e| format!(\"(memory unavailable: {e})\")),\n130\t        Route::EscalateT1 => cascade::run(intent, cascade::FAST).unwrap_or_else(|e| format!(\"(T1 unavailable: {e})\")),\n131\t        Route::EscalateT2 => cascade::run(intent, cascade::DEEP).unwrap_or_else(|e| format!(\"(T2 unavailable: {e})\")),\n132\t        Route::DispatchAgent => format!(\n133\t            \"[dispatch] the agent mesh (ARTIFICER/SCHOLAR/ENVOY) is Phase 3; the KERNEL recorded this intent:\\n  {intent}\"\n134\t        ),\n135\t    };\n136\t    finish(w, \"KERNEL\", tool, intent, &v, &output);\n137\t}\n138\t\n139\tfn cmd_do(w: &Warden, tool: &str, args: &str) {\n140\t    let v = w.decide(tool);\n141\t    println!(\"[direct tool {}]  WARDEN: {} {}\", tool, v.tier, v.decision.as_str());\n142\t    if !v.decision.may_run() {\n143\t        block(w, \"KERNEL\", tool, args, &v);\n144\t        return;\n145\t    }\n146\t    // v1 has no real tool executors (those are the Phase-3 agents) — record an executed stub so\n147\t    // the round-trip + tiering + signed log are demonstrable end-to-end.\n148\t    let output = format!(\"[executed] {tool} {args}\").trim_end().to_string();\n149\t    finish(w, \"KERNEL\", tool, args, &v, &output);\n150\t}\n151\t\n152\tfn finish(w: &Warden, agent: &str, tool: &str, args: &str, v: &Verdict, output: &str) {\n153\t    let rh = format!(\"b3:{}\", &sha256_hex(output.as_bytes())[..12]);\n154\t    match w.record(agent, tool, &args_hash(args), v, \"auto\", &rh, now()) {\n155\t        Ok(rec) => {\n156\t            println!(\"\\n{output}\\n\");\n157\t            println!(\n158\t                \"[WARDEN {} {} — logged @ action spine seq {} · {}]\",\n159\t                v.tier, v.decision.as_str(), rec.seq, &rec.entry_hash[..16]\n160\t            );\n161\t        }\n162\t        Err(e) => eprintln!(\"action-log write failed: {e}\"),\n163\t    }\n164\t}\n165\t\n166\tfn block(w: &Warden, agent: &str, tool: &str, args: &str, v: &Verdict) {\n167\t    let need = if v.tier == Tier::A3 { \"explicit per-action approval (no promotion)\" } else { \"one-tap approval\" };\n168\t    let _ = w.record(agent, tool, &args_hash(args), v, \"none\", \"blocked:awaiting-approval\", now());\n169\t    println!(\"[BLOCKED — {} requires {}; not executed. Logged as awaiting-approval.]\", v.tier, need);\n170\t    anchor_head(w); // protect the evidence that a dangerous action was attempted (anti-rollback)\n171\t}\n172\t\n173\tfn cmd_audit(w: &Warden) {\n174\t    match w.records() {\n175\t        Ok(recs) if recs.is_empty() => println!(\"(action log empty — no actions yet)\"),\n176\t        Ok(recs) => {\n177\t            println!(\"SIGIL action spine — {} record(s), verbatim (C18 self-audit):\\n\", recs.len());\n178\t            for r in recs {\n179\t                println!(\n180\t                    \"  seq {:<3} {:<4} {:<20} {:<10} agent={:<10} approver={:<12} ts={}\",\n181\t                    r.seq, r.tier, r.tool, r.decision, r.agent, r.approver, r.ts\n182\t                );\n183\t            }\n184\t        }\n185\t        Err(e) => eprintln!(\"audit failed: {e}\"),\n186\t    }\n187\t}\n188\t\n189\tfn cmd_verify(w: &Warden, allow_unanchored: bool) {\n190\t    let n = match w.verify() {\n191\t        Ok(n) => n,\n192\t        Err(e) => {\n193\t            eprintln!(\"action log FAIL: {e}\");\n194\t            std::process::exit(2);\n195\t        }\n196\t    };\n197\t    let on_disk = w.head().ok().flatten().map(|(c, _)| c).unwrap_or(0);\n198\t\n199\t    // Cross-check the spine-anchored monotonic high-water. The anchor read itself FAILS CLOSED:\n200\t    // a bridge error, a tampered spine (the bridge exits non-zero), OR an established-but-now-zero\n201\t    // high-water are all treated as UNVERIFIABLE — because the checker (bridge + spine) is under\n202\t    // the same file-write attacker the anchor defends against, resolving its failure as \"OK\" would\n203\t    // hand the attacker a clean bill. `--allow-unanchored` is the explicit escape for a genuine\n204\t    // first run / offline check.\n205\t    match anchor::high_water(&w.public_hex()) {\n206\t        Ok((hw, _)) if on_disk < hw => {\n207\t            eprintln!(\"action log FAIL: ROLLBACK — on-disk head count {on_disk} < spine-anchored high-water {hw} (recent records erased)\");\n208\t            std::process::exit(2);\n209\t        }\n210\t        Ok((hw, _)) if hw > 0 => {\n211\t            println!(\"action log OK: {n} record(s) verify — chain + signatures + anti-rollback anchor (spine high-water {hw})\");\n212\t        }\n213\t        // hw == 0 (never anchored for this key) or Err (bridge/spine unavailable) → unverifiable\n214\t        other => {\n215\t            if allow_unanchored {\n216\t                let why = if other.is_ok() { \"no anchor established yet\" } else { \"anchor bridge unavailable\" };\n217\t                println!(\"action log OK (LOCAL ONLY): {n} record(s) verify — chain + signatures; anti-rollback UNVERIFIED ({why}, --allow-unanchored)\");\n218\t            } else if n == 0 {\n219\t                println!(\"action log OK: empty log, nothing to anchor\");\n220\t            } else {\n221\t                eprintln!(\"action log UNVERIFIABLE: {n} record(s) pass local checks but the anti-rollback anchor is unreachable/unestablished — cannot certify freshness (a rollback+bridge-sabotage looks identical). Re-run with --allow-unanchored only if this is a genuine first run/offline.\");\n222\t                std::process::exit(3);\n223\t            }\n224\t        }\n225\t    }\n226\t}\n227\t"}]},"uuid":"970bae05-b17a-42f9-8acb-05d9ead66fd6","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:48.248Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"51e2b622-758e-4099-9868-413fd49f7d3d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"2bdab5c2-c18e-4c4e-bdef-a82151dae02d","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a9a9282aa914a9b3f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01YSmTvcABbkXiDY3Ra3L1nY","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t//! Cognition cascade dispatch (SIGIL §7). T1/T2 escalations run against Claude via the headless\n2\t//! `claude -p` CLI on the Max plan (the same zero-API-cost path SIGIL uses everywhere) — T1 a\n3\t//! fast model, T2 a deep one. T0 is the router (see `router`); T3 specialists are later phases.\n4\t\n5\tuse std::io;\n6\tuse std::process::Command;\n7\t\n8\tconst CLAUDE: &str = \"/home/kali/.local/bin/claude\";\n9\t\n10\t/// A fast frontier model (Haiku-class) — drafts, triage, short answers.\n11\tpub const FAST: &str = \"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001\";\n12\t/// A deep frontier model (Sonnet-class) — planning, hard reasoning, code.\n13\tpub const DEEP: &str = \"claude-sonnet-5\";\n14\t\n15\t/// Run a prompt through `claude -p --model <model>` and return its text. The KERNEL treats the\n16\t/// output as advisory content; anything with external effect must still cross WARDEN.\n17\tpub fn run(prompt: &str, model: &str) -> io::Result<String> {\n18\t    let out = Command::new(CLAUDE).args([\"-p\", prompt, \"--model\", model]).output()?;\n19\t    if !out.status.success() {\n20\t        return Err(io::Error::new(\n21\t            io::ErrorKind::Other,\n22\t            String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr).lines().last().unwrap_or(\"claude error\").to_string(),\n23\t        ));\n24\t    }\n25\t    Ok(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout).trim().to_string())\n26\t}\n27\t"}]},"uuid":"923ee5e8-d4dc-4d56-b3d4-c0429737d975","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:54.902Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"2bdab5c2-c18e-4c4e-bdef-a82151dae02d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"30ee162d-63e7-4dc2-9c7b-95bd9863c03d","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a9a9282aa914a9b3f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01G8A4d5vd8bGFpS9q3A5dGj","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t//! T0 cognition router (SIGIL §7). T0 classifies every input into one of four routes. This v1\n2\t//! is deterministic + rule-based (no model call, <1 ms) — a small local Ollama model is a later\n3\t//! refinement. The router policy is DATA and is versioned like code (SIGIL §7).\n4\t\n5\t#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]\n6\tpub enum Route {\n7\t    /// Answer directly from memory (a recall question). Fast path.\n8\t    AnswerLocal,\n9\t    /// Escalate to a fast frontier model (Haiku-class): short drafts/triage.\n10\t    EscalateT1,\n11\t    /// Escalate to a deep frontier model (Sonnet/Opus-class): planning, hard reasoning, code.\n12\t    EscalateT2,\n13\t    /// Dispatch to an agent (build/research/comms) — real mesh is Phase 3.\n14\t    DispatchAgent,\n15\t}\n16\t\n17\timpl Route {\n18\t    pub fn as_str(&self) -> &'static str {\n19\t        match self {\n20\t            Route::AnswerLocal => \"answer_local\",\n21\t            Route::EscalateT1 => \"escalate_t1\",\n22\t            Route::EscalateT2 => \"escalate_t2\",\n23\t            Route::DispatchAgent => \"dispatch_agent\",\n24\t        }\n25\t    }\n26\t}\n27\t\n28\tconst MEMORY_CUES: &[&str] = &[\n29\t    \"what did i\", \"when did i\", \"recall\", \"remember when\", \"what do i know\", \"search memory\",\n30\t    \"what did we\", \"who is\", \"show me\", \"find in memory\", \"history of\", \"what have i decided\",\n31\t    \"did i decide\", \"what's open\", \"what is open\", \"what did you do\",\n32\t];\n33\tconst AGENT_CUES: &[&str] = &[\n34\t    \"build \", \"implement \", \"fix the\", \"open a pr\", \"refactor\", \"run the tests\", \"deploy\",\n35\t    \"research \", \"compare \", \"due diligence\", \"draft an email\", \"send an email\", \"email \",\n36\t];\n37\tconst PLAN_CUES: &[&str] = &[\n38\t    \"plan \", \"design \", \"architect\", \"how should i\", \"strategy\", \"reason about\", \"think through\",\n39\t    \"write code\", \"debug \", \"why does\",\n40\t];\n41\t\n42\t/// Route an intent. Order: memory-recall → agent-task → deep-plan → (short ⇒ T1, else T2).\n43\tpub fn route(intent: &str) -> Route {\n44\t    let t = intent.to_ascii_lowercase();\n45\t    if MEMORY_CUES.iter().any(|c| t.contains(c)) {\n46\t        return Route::AnswerLocal;\n47\t    }\n48\t    if AGENT_CUES.iter().any(|c| t.contains(c)) {\n49\t        return Route::DispatchAgent;\n50\t    }\n51\t    if PLAN_CUES.iter().any(|c| t.contains(c)) {\n52\t        return Route::EscalateT2;\n53\t    }\n54\t    if t.split_whitespace().count() <= 12 {\n55\t        Route::EscalateT1\n56\t    } else {\n57\t        Route::EscalateT2\n58\t    }\n59\t}\n60\t\n61\t#[cfg(test)]\n62\tmod tests {\n63\t    use super::*;\n64\t\n65\t    #[test]\n66\t    fn routes_match_intent_class() {\n67\t        assert_eq!(route(\"what did i decide about the graph database\"), Route::AnswerLocal);\n68\t        assert_eq!(route(\"recall my reasons for choosing Qdrant\"), Route::AnswerLocal);\n69\t        assert_eq!(route(\"build a rate limiter for the API\"), Route::DispatchAgent);\n70\t        assert_eq!(route(\"research vector databases for on-device use\"), Route::DispatchAgent);\n71\t        assert_eq!(route(\"design the WARDEN tier promotion policy\"), Route::EscalateT2);\n72\t        assert_eq!(route(\"summarize this in a line\"), Route::EscalateT1); // short, no cue\n73\t        assert_eq!(\n74\t            route(\"give me a careful multi-paragraph comparison of the tradeoffs between these three approaches in detail\"),\n75\t            Route::EscalateT2 // long, no cue\n76\t        );\n77\t    }\n78\t}\n79\t"}]},"uuid":"338bc5e8-446e-4332-912c-81f88ea2d1ea","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:55.366Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"30ee162d-63e7-4dc2-9c7b-95bd9863c03d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"746aba45-c4ff-416f-a05b-be6d51157777","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a9a9282aa914a9b3f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01WP9UEnKx9P7J2dUPXkg3nK","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t//! Memory bridge — the KERNEL reaches the Phase-0 memory substrate (the Python `sigil` package)\n2\t//! by shelling out to its CLI. {"parentUuid":"7c5db68d-2a17-446f-9615-afaf306e7d9c","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a9a9282aa914a9b3f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01QcRYwx4NPqdXDiiE4RCTgF","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Approval queue (SIGIL §5, §9.3 mobile bridge) — the human gate for A2/A3 proposals. An agent\n2\tQUEUES a proposal (governor → QUEUE); the owner APPROVES or DENIES it here. Authentication is an\n3\tEd25519 signature over the canonical (target, decision, approver) by the OWNER key.\n4\t\n5\tRED-PEN HARDENING (Phase 6):\n6\t • The trusted pubkey is the PERSISTED owner identity — NOT the key supplied to the queue, so an\n7\t   attacker cannot self-certify by bringing their own key (RP-APPROVAL-2). approve/deny require the\n8\t   supplied signing key to BE the trusted owner key.\n9\t • `pending()` treats a queued item as resolved ONLY when a superseding approval VERIFIES against the\n10\t   trusted owner key, and keys resolution off the SIGNED target_seq (not the raw supersedes_id) — so\n11\t   an unsigned/forged approval can't silently drop an item (RP-1/RP-APPROVAL-1), and a genuine\n12\t   approval of one item can't be replayed onto another (RP-APPROVAL-4). `verify_approval` is thereby\n13\t   on the enforcement path (RP-APPROVAL-3).\n14\t\n15\tThis records the DECISION; it executes no external effect (SIGIL has no send/deploy path). The\n16\ttransport (Telegram/WhatsApp over WireGuard, §9.3) is a seam over this authenticated core.\"\"\"\n17\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n18\t\n19\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n20\t\n21\tfrom ..reuse import canonical_json, sha256_hex, sign, verify_one\n22\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n23\t\n24\tSIGNAL = \"governor.approval\"\n25\t_QUEUED_STATUS = \"awaiting-approval\"\n26\t\n27\t\n28\tdef _approval_message(target_seq, decision: str, approver: str) -> bytes:\n29\t    m = canonical_json({\"target\": target_seq, \"decision\": decision, \"approver\": approver})\n30\t    return m if isinstance(m, bytes) else m.encode()\n31\t\n32\t\n33\tdef verify_approval(record, trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str]) -> bool:\n34\t    \"\"\"True iff the approval carries a valid OWNER signature over its (target, decision, approver).\n35\t    Unsigned, wrong-key, or no-trusted-key → False (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n36\t    p = record.payload if hasattr(record, \"payload\") else record\n37\t    sig = p.get(\"sig\")\n38\t    if not sig or not trusted_pubkey_b64 or p.get(\"pubkey\") != trusted_pubkey_b64:\n39\t        return False\n40\t    msg = _approval_message(p.get(\"target_seq\"), p.get(\"approval\"), p.get(\"approver\"))\n41\t    return verify_one(trusted_pubkey_b64, msg, sig)\n42\t\n43\t\n44\tdef pending(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str] = None) -> List:\n45\t    \"\"\"Queued proposals with NO valid owner approval yet, oldest first. Resolution requires a\n46\t    VERIFIED approval; an unsigned/forged/wrong-key approval leaves the item pending (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n47\t    if trusted_pubkey_b64 is None:\n48\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n49\t        trusted_pubkey_b64 = owner_pubkey()\n50\t    resolved = set()\n51\t    queued = {}\n52\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n53\t        p = r.payload\n54\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == SIGNAL and verify_approval(r, trusted_pubkey_b64):\n55\t            resolved.add(p.get(\"target_seq\"))       # honor the SIGNED target, not raw supersedes_id\n56\t        if p.get(\"decision\") == \"queued\" and p.get(\"status\") == _QUEUED_STATUS:\n57\t            queued[r.seq] = r\n58\t    return [r for seq, r in sorted(queued.items()) if seq not in resolved]\n59\t\n60\t\n61\tclass ApprovalError(Exception):\n62\t    pass\n63\t\n64\t\n65\tclass ApprovalQueue:\n66\t    def __init__(self, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, owner_key=None,\n67\t                 trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str] = None):\n68\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n69\t        self.store = store or SpineStore()\n70\t        self.owner_key = owner_key if owner_key is not None else owner_keypair()\n71\t        # PINNED to the persisted owner identity — never defaulted to the supplied key (RP-APPROVAL-2)\n72\t        self.trusted_pubkey_b64 = trusted_pubkey_b64 if trusted_pubkey_b64 is not None else owner_pubkey()\n73\t\n74\t    def _target(self, seq: int):\n75\t        for r in pending(self.store, self.trusted_pubkey_b64):\n76\t            if r.seq == seq:\n77\t                return r\n78\t        raise ApprovalError(f\"seq {seq} is not a pending approval (already decided, or not queued)\")\n79\t\n80\t    def _decide(self, seq: int, decision: str, *, approver: str, reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n81\t        target = self._target(seq)\n82\t        # the signing key MUST be the trusted owner key — not merely \"some key present\" (RP-APPROVAL-2).\n83\t        if (self.owner_key is None or self.trusted_pubkey_b64 is None\n84\t                or self.owner_key.public_key_b64 != self.trusted_pubkey_b64):\n85\t            raise ApprovalError(\"approval requires the OWNER's signing key (missing or not the trusted key)\")\n86\t        msg = _approval_message(seq, decision, approver)\n87\t        sig = sign(self.owner_key.private_key_b64, msg)\n88\t        payload = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"approval\": decision, \"target_seq\": seq, \"target_kind\": target.kind,\n89\t                   \"target_tier\": target.payload.get(\"tier\"), \"approver\": approver, \"reason\": reason,\n90\t                   \"pubkey\": self.trusted_pubkey_b64, \"sig\": sig, \"msg_digest\": sha256_hex(msg),\n91\t                   \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n92\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"OWNER\",\n93\t                                 payload=payload, supersedes_id=seq)\n94\t\n95\t    def approve(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n96\t        return self._decide(seq, \"approved\", approver=approver, reason=reason)\n97\t\n98\t    def deny(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n99\t        return self._decide(seq, \"denied\", approver=approver, reason=reason)\n100\t"}]},"uuid":"6cb99f22-b352-4003-9d4b-e24c0ba132cb","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:39:09.729Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"7c5db68d-2a17-446f-9615-afaf306e7d9c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"9b22cbd3-8ae0-4e63-80b3-7555ccaa17ea","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a9a9282aa914a9b3f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01S2rEJSaGNdR1LyAQgZN5oj","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"ENVOY (SIGIL §4.6) — communications & relationships: triage inbound, DRAFT outbound, track\n2\topen loops. Ceiling A2 HARD, and NO PROMOTION PATH — outbound stays human-gated permanently.\n3\t\n4\tThis is enforced STRUCTURALLY: ENVOY has no method that transmits anything. It only classifies\n5\tinbound and writes `draft` records (A2 → queued, `status: awaiting-approval`). A human reads the\n6\tdraft and sends it (or not). There is deliberately no `send()`. The inbox is a pluggable source\n7\t(a file of messages for tests/offline; IMAP is an optional real source, not built here).\"\"\"\n8\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n9\t\n10\timport json\n11\tfrom pathlib import Path\n12\tfrom typing import List, Protocol, runtime_checkable\n13\t\n14\tfrom .base import Agent, AgentResult, Proposal, Tier\n15\t\n16\t_SPAM = (\"unsubscribe\", \"viagra\", \"lottery\", \"prince\", \"crypto giveaway\", \"you have won\", \"click here to claim\")\n17\t_URGENT = (\"urgent\", \"asap\", \"as soon as possible\", \"deadline\", \"today\", \"emergency\", \"overdue\", \"past due\", \"final notice\")\n18\t_FYI = (\"no-reply\", \"noreply\", \"newsletter\", \"notification\", \"digest\", \"do not reply\")\n19\t\n20\t\n21\t@runtime_checkable\n22\tclass InboxSource(Protocol):\n23\t    def messages(self) -> List[dict]: ...   # each: {from, subject, body, date}\n24\t\n25\t\n26\tclass FileInbox:\n27\t    \"\"\"A JSON file of messages — for tests/offline. Real IMAP is a separate optional source.\"\"\"\n28\t    def __init__(self, path: str):\n29\t        self.path = Path(path)\n30\t\n31\t    def messages(self) -> List[dict]:\n32\t        if not self.path.exists():\n33\t            return []\n34\t        try:\n35\t            data = json.loads(self.path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n36\t        except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError):\n37\t            return []   # a corrupt/truncated inbox degrades to empty, never crashes the triage pass\n38\t        return data if isinstance(data, list) else data.get(\"messages\", [])\n39\t\n40\t\n41\tdef triage(msg: dict) -> str:\n42\t    \"\"\"Classify a message → urgent | normal | fyi | spam (deterministic, keyword-based).\"\"\"\n43\t    sender = str(msg.get(\"from\", \"\")).lower()\n44\t    text = f\"{msg.get('subject', '')} {msg.get('body', '')}\".lower()\n45\t    if any(k in text for k in _SPAM):\n46\t        return \"spam\"\n47\t    if any(k in sender for k in _FYI):\n48\t        return \"fyi\"\n49\t    if any(k in text for k in _URGENT):\n50\t        return \"urgent\"\n51\t    return \"normal\"\n52\t\n53\t\n54\tdef draft_reply(msg: dict) -> str:\n55\t    \"\"\"A conservative acknowledgement DRAFT (never sent). A real deployment can swap in an\n56\t    agent-composed draft via the cognition cascade; the doctrine (drafts-only) is unchanged.\"\"\"\n57\t    who = str(msg.get(\"from\", \"there\")).split(\"@\")[0].split(\"<\")[0].strip() or \"there\"\n58\t    subj = msg.get(\"subject\", \"your message\")\n59\t    return (f\"Hi {who},\\n\\nThanks for your message regarding \\\"{subj}\\\" — I've received it and \"\n60\t            f\"will follow up shortly.\\n\\n[DRAFT — SIGIL/ENVOY. Review and send manually; nothing is sent automatically.]\")\n61\t\n62\t\n63\tclass Envoy(Agent):\n64\t    name = \"ENVOY\"\n65\t    mandate = \"triage inbound, DRAFT outbound (never send), track open loops\"\n66\t    ceiling = Tier.A2  # hard — no promotion to auto, ever\n67\t\n68\t    def run(self, inbox: InboxSource) -> AgentResult:\n69\t        proposals: List[Proposal] = []\n70\t        open_loops = 0\n71\t        for msg in inbox.messages():\n72\t            cls = triage(msg)\n73\t            # the triage note is a reversible internal record → A1, auto.\n74\t            proposals.append(Proposal(\"interaction\", {\n75\t                \"from\": msg.get(\"from\"), \"subject\": msg.get(\"subject\"),\n76\t                \"triage\": cls, \"date\": msg.get(\"date\")}, Tier.A1))\n77\t            if cls in (\"urgent\", \"normal\"):\n78\t                open_loops += 1\n79\t                # the reply is a DRAFT — A2, ALWAYS queued (never auto-sent).\n80\t                proposals.append(Proposal(\"draft\", {\n81\t                    \"to\": msg.get(\"from\"), \"subject\": f\"Re: {msg.get('subject', '')}\",\n82\t                    \"body\": draft_reply(msg), \"in_reply_to\": msg.get(\"subject\"), \"triage\": cls}, Tier.A2))\n83\t        res = self._dispatch(proposals)\n84\t        res.notes.append(f\"triaged {len(inbox.messages())} messages; {open_loops} open loop(s) drafted (awaiting approval)\")\n85\t        return res\n86\t\n87\t    # NOTE: there is intentionally no send()/transmit() method. Outbound is human-only, forever.\n88\t"}]},"uuid":"0a2a20cf-0125-41dc-b437-a176a523ec32","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:39:10.257Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"9b22cbd3-8ae0-4e63-80b3-7555ccaa17ea","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"c0bd09e9-296b-40a3-8fae-0399d8e48a72","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a9a9282aa914a9b3f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01KopUgqnr1ZquQVaMwQnRdZ","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t//! WARDEN autonomy tiers (SIGIL §5). Every tool invocation is classified A0–A3; the gate\n2\t//! decision follows from the tier. FAIL-CLOSED and TOKEN-BASED: the tool name is split into\n3\t//! whole tokens (on `.`/`_`/`-`/`/`/space) and matched against whole-token sets — never a raw\n4\t//! substring, so \"overwrite\" is not \"write\" and \"forget\" is not \"get\". Danger is checked FIRST,\n5\t//! and A0 (auto, un-queued) is reachable ONLY via a positive safe-verb allowlist; anything not\n6\t//! positively classified — including any unknown or dangerous-target tool — is A3.\n7\t\n8\tuse std::fmt;\n9\t\n10\tuse serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\n11\t\n12\t#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n13\tpub enum Tier {\n14\t    /// Observe / answer — memory queries, screen read, voice reply. Auto.\n15\t    A0,\n16\t    /// Reversible internal act — write a report/brief, draft-branch, raise an alert. Auto + logged.\n17\t    A1,\n18\t    /// External-visible / semi-reversible — send email, calendar write, purchase < cap. Queued.\n19\t    A2,\n20\t    /// Destructive / financial / security — push protected branch, deploy, delete, spend > cap. Explicit.\n21\t    A3,\n22\t}\n23\t\n24\timpl Tier {\n25\t    pub fn as_str(&self) -> &'static str {\n26\t        match self {\n27\t            Tier::A0 => \"A0\",\n28\t            Tier::A1 => \"A1\",\n29\t            Tier::A2 => \"A2\",\n30\t            Tier::A3 => \"A3\",\n31\t        }\n32\t    }\n33\t}\n34\t\n35\timpl fmt::Display for Tier {\n36\t    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {\n37\t        f.write_str(self.as_str())\n38\t    }\n39\t}\n40\t\n41\t#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n42\tpub enum Decision {\n43\t    Auto,\n44\t    Queued,\n45\t    ExplicitRequired,\n46\t}\n47\t\n48\timpl Decision {\n49\t    pub fn as_str(&self) -> &'static str {\n50\t        match self {\n51\t            Decision::Auto => \"auto\",\n52\t            Decision::Queued => \"queued\",\n53\t            Decision::ExplicitRequired => \"explicit-required\",\n54\t        }\n55\t    }\n56\t    pub fn may_run(&self) -> bool {\n57\t        matches!(self, Decision::Auto)\n58\t    }\n59\t}\n60\t\n61\t/// A3 — destructive verbs, financial ops, crypto/restore ops, and DANGEROUS TARGETS\n62\t/// (secret/identity/network/prod material is A3 regardless of the verb: `secrets.read`,\n63\t/// `iam.policy.write`, `budget.get`, `vault.read`).\n64\tconst A3_TOKENS: &[&str] = &[\n65\t    // destructive verbs\n66\t    \"push\", \"deploy\", \"delete\", \"destroy\", \"drop\", \"remove\", \"purge\", \"wipe\", \"truncate\",\n67\t    \"overwrite\", \"erase\", \"format\", \"kill\", \"shutdown\", \"reboot\", \"reset\", \"disable\", \"enable\",\n68\t    \"override\", \"force\", \"sudo\", \"exec\", \"eval\", \"chmod\", \"chown\", \"patch\", \"install\", \"uninstall\",\n69\t    // crypto + restore/rollback (a restore silently reverts current state)\n70\t    \"encrypt\", \"decrypt\", \"restore\", \"revert\", \"rollback\", \"recover\",\n71\t    // financial\n72\t    \"spend\", \"purchase\", \"pay\", \"payment\", \"transaction\", \"transfer\", \"refund\", \"invoice\",\n73\t    \"allocate\", \"budget\", \"release\", \"sign\",\n74\t    // security / identity / infra / secret-store TARGETS (dangerous no matter the verb)\n75\t    \"secret\", \"secrets\", \"credential\", \"credentials\", \"token\", \"tokens\", \"key\", \"keys\", \"iam\",\n76\t    \"policy\", \"firewall\", \"acl\", \"role\", \"grant\", \"revoke\", \"rotate\", \"escalate\", \"infra\",\n77\t    \"prod\", \"production\", \"env\", \"master\", \"root\", \"admin\",\n78\t    \"vault\", \"keyring\", \"keychain\", \"keystore\", \"hsm\",\n79\t];\n80\t\n81\t/// A2 — external-visible / semi-reversible: communication, publishing, and bulk DATA EGRESS\n82\t/// (export/dump of a private store must be human-seen, never auto).\n83\tconst A2_TOKENS: &[&str] = &[\n84\t    \"send\", \"email\", \"smtp\", \"publish\", \"post\", \"message\", \"outbound\", \"webhook\", \"sms\", \"notify\",\n85\t    \"share\", \"upload\", \"invite\", \"calendar\", \"tweet\", \"dm\", \"export\", \"dump\", \"download\", \"sync\",\n86\t];\n87\t\n88\t/// A1 — reversible internal writes (drafts, notes, memory writes, non-push commits). NOTE:\n89\t/// \"snapshot\" is intentionally NOT here — a snapshot RESTORE is destructive (caught by A3\n90\t/// \"restore\"), and snapshot-create alone falls to fail-closed A3 rather than risk auto-run.\n91\tconst A1_TOKENS: &[&str] = &[\n92\t    \"write\", \"note\", \"brief\", \"report\", \"draft\", \"alert\", \"consolidate\", \"commit\", \"branch\",\n93\t    \"annotate\", \"tag\", \"label\",\n94\t];\n95\t\n96\t/// A0 — POSITIVE allowlist of known-safe observe/answer VERBS only (NO target nouns — a target\n97\t/// noun here would let e.g. `memory.encrypt` auto-run). A tool reaches A0 only by a read-verb\n98\t/// here, or an exact name in `A0_TOOLS`; absence of danger alone is NOT sufficient → fail-closed A3.\n99\tconst A0_VERBS: &[&str] = &[\n100\t    \"read\", \"search\", \"query\", \"get\", \"list\", \"status\", \"recall\", \"observe\", \"answer\",\n101\t    \"view\", \"show\", \"find\", \"frame\", \"peek\", \"describe\", \"inspect\", \"lookup\",\n102\t];\n103\t\n104\t/// A0 — exact-name allowlist for the known read-only memory tools whose names are not verb-clean\n105\t/// (`graph.entity`, `episodic.range`, `threads.open`, `commitments.due`, `contradictions.pending`,\n106\t/// `ingest.status`). Matched on the whole lowercased tool name.\n107\tconst A0_TOOLS: &[&str] = &[\n108\t    \"memory.search\", \"graph.query\", \"graph.entity\", \"episodic.range\", \"ingest.status\",\n109\t    \"threads.open\", \"commitments.due\", \"contradictions.pending\",\n110\t];\n111\t\n112\t/// Split a tool name into whole tokens on `.`/`_`/`-`/`/` and whitespace, lowercased.\n113\tfn tokens(tool: &str) -> Vec<String> {\n114\t    tool.split(|c: char| c == '.' || c == '_' || c == '-' || c == '/' || c.is_whitespace())\n115\t        .filter(|s| !s.is_empty())\n116\t        .map(|s| s.to_ascii_lowercase())\n117\t        .collect()\n118\t}\n119\t\n120\t/// Classify a tool name into a tier. Danger FIRST, then A2, A1; A0 only via a positive safe-verb\n121\t/// or an exact safe-tool name; everything else (unknown / not-positively-safe / empty) → A3.\n122\tpub fn classify(tool: &str) -> Tier {\n123\t    let full = tool.to_ascii_lowercase();\n124\t    let tk = tokens(tool);\n125\t    if tk.is_empty() {\n126\t        return Tier::A3;\n127\t    }\n128\t    let has = |set: &[&str]| tk.iter().any(|t| set.contains(&t.as_str()));\n129\t    if has(A3_TOKENS) {\n130\t        return Tier::A3;\n131\t    }\n132\t    if has(A2_TOKENS) {\n133\t        return Tier::A2;\n134\t    }\n135\t    if has(A1_TOKENS) {\n136\t        return Tier::A1;\n137\t    }\n138\t    if A0_TOOLS.contains(&full.as_str()) || has(A0_VERBS) {\n139\t        return Tier::A0;\n140\t    }\n141\t    Tier::A3 // not positively classified → fail-closed to the most-gated tier\n142\t}\n143\t\n144\tpub fn gate(tier: Tier) -> Decision {\n145\t    match tier {\n146\t        Tier::A0 | Tier::A1 => Decision::Auto,\n147\t        Tier::A2 => Decision::Queued,\n148\t        Tier::A3 => Decision::ExplicitRequired,\n149\t    }\n150\t}\n151\t\n152\t#[cfg(test)]\n153\tmod tests {\n154\t    use super::*;\n155\t\n156\t    #[test]\n157\t    fn classification_matches_spec_examples() {\n158\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"memory.search\"), Tier::A0);\n159\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"graph.query\"), Tier::A0);\n160\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"memory.write\"), Tier::A1);\n161\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"brief.compose\"), Tier::A1);\n162\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"email.send\"), Tier::A2);\n163\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"calendar.write\"), Tier::A2); // \"calendar\" (A2) beats \"write\" (A1)\n164\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"git.push\"), Tier::A3);\n165\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"deploy.prod\"), Tier::A3);\n166\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"data.delete\"), Tier::A3);\n167\t    }\n168\t\n169\t    #[test]\n170\t    fn unknown_and_empty_are_fail_closed_a3() {\n171\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"something.unclassified\"), Tier::A3);\n172\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"\"), Tier::A3);\n173\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"weird\"), Tier::A3);\n174\t        assert_eq!(gate(classify(\"something.unclassified\")), Decision::ExplicitRequired);\n175\t    }\n176\t\n177\t    #[test]\n178\t    fn substring_collisions_do_not_lower_the_tier() {\n179\t        // the exact review findings: a dangerous op must NOT ride a benign substring down a tier\n180\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"config.overwrite\"), Tier::A3, \"overwrite != write\");\n181\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"db.overwrite\"), Tier::A3);\n182\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"files.forget\"), Tier::A3, \"forget != get; fail-closed\");\n183\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"budget.override\"), Tier::A3, \"financial + override\");\n184\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"budget.get\"), Tier::A3, \"financial target is A3 regardless of verb\");\n185\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"iam.policy.write\"), Tier::A3, \"identity write is A3, not A1\");\n186\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"firewall.rule.write\"), Tier::A3);\n187\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"transaction.sign\"), Tier::A3, \"financial signing is A3, not A1\");\n188\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"secrets.read\"), Tier::A3, \"secret read is A3, not A0\");\n189\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"credentials.get\"), Tier::A3);\n190\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"account.disable\"), Tier::A3);\n191\t    }\n192\t\n193\t    #[test]\n194\t    fn dangerous_ops_on_safe_targets_do_not_auto_run() {\n195\t        // the CRITICAL re-check finding: a target noun must NOT grant A0 to a dangerous verb\n196\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"memory.encrypt\"), Tier::A3, \"ransomware-shaped op must NOT be A0\");\n197\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"graph.destroy\"), Tier::A3);\n198\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"entity.merge\"), Tier::A3, \"irreversible rewrite → fail-closed, not A0\");\n199\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"memory.export\"), Tier::A2, \"full-store egress must be queued, not auto\");\n200\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"memory.dump\"), Tier::A2);\n201\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"snapshot.restore\"), Tier::A3, \"a restore reverts state → A3\");\n202\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"vault.read\"), Tier::A3, \"secret-store read is A3 regardless of verb\");\n203\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"keyring.get\"), Tier::A3);\n204\t    }\n205\t\n206\t    #[test]\n207\t    fn safe_memory_tools_still_reach_a0() {\n208\t        for t in [\n209\t            \"memory.search\", \"graph.query\", \"graph.entity\", \"episodic.range\", \"ingest.status\",\n210\t            \"threads.open\", \"commitments.due\", \"contradictions.pending\",\n211\t        ] {\n212\t            assert_eq!(classify(t), Tier::A0, \"{t} is a read-only memory tool → A0\");\n213\t        }\n214\t    }\n215\t\n216\t    #[test]\n217\t    fn danger_token_wins_over_safe_token_regardless_of_order() {\n218\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"read.then.delete\"), Tier::A3);\n219\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"delete.and.list\"), Tier::A3);\n220\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"search.and.deploy\"), Tier::A3);\n221\t    }\n222\t\n223\t    #[test]\n224\t    fn gates_are_correct() {\n225\t        assert!(gate(Tier::A0).may_run());\n226\t        assert!(gate(Tier::A1).may_run());\n227\t        assert!(!gate(Tier::A2).may_run());\n228\t        assert!(!gate(Tier::A3).may_run());\n229\t {"parentUuid":"6acf71d9-5b5c-4afb-963a-60a1ce3e543a","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a9a9282aa914a9b3f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01X52gBf6p2ZUennnT3rGM7f","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The SIGIL agent mesh framework (SIGIL §4/§5). Each agent has a MANDATE and an AUTONOMY\n2\tCEILING (A0–A3); everything it does is a `Proposal` carrying a tier. Proposals at or below the\n3\tauto bar (A1) and at or below the agent's ceiling are AUTO-APPLIED (written to the spine);\n4\tanything higher is QUEUED as a pending record a human must approve — never auto-executed.\n5\t\n6\tThe doctrine is enforced STRUCTURALLY, not by discipline: an agent literally cannot take an\n7\texternal-effect action, because no agent has a send/deploy/spend code path. ENVOY, in\n8\tparticular, drafts only — its ceiling is A2 and there is no method that transmits. Agent output\n9\tis provenance-linked spine records, so a brief or a draft is auditable back to what produced it.\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\n13\tfrom enum import IntEnum\n14\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n15\t\n16\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n17\t\n18\tAGENTS_SOURCE = \"agent\"\n19\t\n20\t\n21\tclass Tier(IntEnum):\n22\t    \"\"\"Mirror of the WARDEN tiers (SIGIL §5), ordered so `<=` compares autonomy.\"\"\"\n23\t    A0 = 0   # observe / answer\n24\t    A1 = 1   # reversible internal act (write a report/brief/event) — the AUTO bar\n25\t    A2 = 2   # external-visible / semi-reversible (send, calendar write) — QUEUED\n26\t    A3 = 3   # destructive / financial / security — explicit, never auto\n27\t\n28\t    def label(self) -> str:\n29\t        return self.name\n30\t\n31\t\n32\tAUTO_BAR = Tier.A1  # A0/A1 auto-apply; A2/A3 queue for human approval\n33\t\n34\t\n35\t@dataclass\n36\tclass Proposal:\n37\t    \"\"\"One thing an agent wants to do: emit a record (auto) or take a gated action (queued).\"\"\"\n38\t    kind: str                       # spine record kind (event/finding/brief/interaction/draft/...)\n39\t    payload: dict\n40\t    tier: Tier = Tier.A1\n41\t    parent_id: Optional[int] = None\n42\t    supersedes_id: Optional[int] = None   # this record supersedes an earlier one (e.g. a resolution)\n43\t\n44\t\n45\t@dataclass\n46\tclass AgentResult:\n47\t    agent: str\n48\t    applied: List[int] = field(default_factory=list)     # seqs written (auto)\n49\t    queued: List[dict] = field(default_factory=list)      # proposals held for approval\n50\t    notes: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)\n51\t\n52\t\n53\tclass Agent:\n54\t    \"\"\"Base agent. Subclasses implement `run()`; the base routes every Proposal through the GOVERNOR\n55\t    (SIGIL §5: kill switch, budgets, promotion — Phase 6) and records the outcome on the spine.\n56\t    Backward compatible: with no kill/cap/promotion the governor reproduces the original gate.\"\"\"\n57\t    name: str = \"AGENT\"\n58\t    mandate: str = \"\"\n59\t    ceiling: Tier = Tier.A1\n60\t\n61\t    def __init__(self, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, governor=None):\n62\t        self.store = store or SpineStore()\n63\t        if governor is None:\n64\t            from ..governor import Governor\n65\t            governor = Governor(self.store)\n66\t        self.governor = governor\n67\t\n68\t    def run(self) -> AgentResult:\n69\t        raise NotImplementedError\n70\t\n71\t    def _dispatch(self, proposals: List[Proposal]) -> AgentResult:\n72\t        from ..governor import Outcome\n73\t        res = AgentResult(agent=self.name)\n74\t        for p in proposals:\n75\t            effective = max(p.tier, Tier.A0)\n76\t            # promotion scope = the RECORD KIND (the real action written), not a self-asserted label:\n77\t            # a promotion is \"this agent may auto-approve A2 records of this kind\" (red-pen RP-3).\n78\t            decision = self.governor.decide(agent=self.name, tier=effective,\n79\t                                            ceiling=self.ceiling, scope=p.kind)\n80\t            common = {**p.payload, \"agent\": self.name, \"tier\": effective.label(),\n81\t                      \"governor\": decision.reason}\n82\t            if decision.outcome == Outcome.AUTO:\n83\t                seq = self.store.append(kind=p.kind, source=AGENTS_SOURCE, actor=self.name,\n84\t                                        payload={**common, \"decision\": \"auto\"},\n85\t                                        parent_id=p.parent_id, supersedes_id=p.supersedes_id)\n86\t                res.applied.append(seq)\n87\t            elif decision.outcome == Outcome.QUEUE:\n88\t                seq = self.store.append(kind=p.kind, source=AGENTS_SOURCE, actor=self.name,\n89\t                                        payload={**common, \"decision\": \"queued\", \"status\": \"awaiting-approval\"},\n90\t                                        parent_id=p.parent_id, supersedes_id=p.supersedes_id)\n91\t                res.queued.append({\"seq\": seq, \"kind\": p.kind, \"tier\": effective.label(),\n92\t                                   \"subject\": p.payload.get(\"subject\") or p.payload.get(\"to\")})\n93\t            else:  # DENY — kill switch or budget; record the refusal, take no action\n94\t                self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=AGENTS_SOURCE, actor=self.name,\n95\t                                  payload={\"agent\": self.name, \"tier\": effective.label(),\n96\t                                           \"decision\": \"denied\", \"governor\": decision.reason,\n97\t                                           \"of_kind\": p.kind, \"subject\": p.payload.get(\"subject\")},\n98\t                                  parent_id=p.parent_id)\n99\t                res.notes.append(f\"DENIED {p.kind} ({effective.label()}): {decision.reason}\")\n100\t        return res\n101\t"}]},"uuid":"49787a9d-5784-4aee-b2e4-b69d0ea50b86","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:39:10.987Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"6acf71d9-5b5c-4afb-963a-60a1ce3e543a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

perception + memory read stay alive\")\n302:def cmd_audit(a) -> None:\n309:def cmd_approve(a) -> None:\n322:def cmd_dashboard(a) -> None:\n327:def cmd_verify(a) -> None:\n335:def cmd_status(a) -> None:\n347:    sub = p.add_subparsers(dest=\"cmd\", required=True)\n348:    pi = sub.add_parser(\"ingest\")\n359:    sub.add_parser(\"index\").set_defaults(fn=cmd_index)\n360:    sub.add_parser(\"sign\").set_defaults(fn=cmd_sign)\n361:    ps = sub.add_parser(\"search\"); ps.add_argument(\"query\"); ps.add_argument(\"-k\", type=int, default=8); ps.set_defaults(fn=cmd_search)\n362:    pg = sub.add_parser(\"graph\")\n367:    pcon = sub.add_parser(\"consolidate\")\n378:    pwas = sub.add_parser(\"warden-anchor-set\")\n384:    pwag = sub.add_parser(\"warden-anchor-get\")\n387:    pa = sub.add_parser(\"agents\")\n389:                                       \"artifice\", \"bastion\", \"perceive\"])\n403:    pv = sub.add_parser(\"voice\")\n405:    pv.add_argument(\"--out\", default=\"/tmp/sigil-voice-out.wav\", help=\"output WAV (file mode)\")\n410:                    help=\"elevenlabs=cloud TTS (JARVIS voice); piper=local; silence=fallback\")\n412:    pv.add_argument(\"--tts-voice\", dest=\"tts_voice\", default=None,\n413:                    help=\"voice_id (ElevenLabs) or .onnx (Piper); default = pinned SIGIL_TTS_VOICE_ID\")\n414:    pv.add_argument(\"--find-voice\", dest=\"find_voice\", metavar=\"QUERY\",\n415:                    help=\"search the ElevenLabs library (e.g. 'jarvis') and list voice_ids\")\n416:    pv.add_argument(\"--set-voice\", dest=\"set_voice\", metavar=\"VOICE_ID\",\n417:                    help=\"pin a TTS voice_id as the default (SIGIL_TTS_VOICE_ID)\")\n418:    pv.set_defaults(fn=cmd_voice)\n419:    pwd = sub.add_parser(\"warden\", help=\"governor controls (kill switch, promotion policy)\")\n425:    pau = sub.add_parser(\"audit\", help=\"self-audit (C18): what the mesh did and why, from the log\")\n429:        pap = sub.add_parser(name, help=f\"{name} a queued A2/A3 proposal by seq (owner-signed)\")\n434:    sub.add_parser(\"dashboard\", help=\"read-only operator status over the spine\").set_defaults(fn=cmd_dashboard)","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"5659cc64-4fb6-467c-8a67-1bd55517a55b","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:39:28.568Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"488127a5-e3d7-45a5-9b36-09adee2135e8","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"aa129442-d97d-487a-b343-1bf48ad89420","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a9a9282aa914a9b3f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01VLNmUVZSL1SH7cZopmKeY6","type":"tool_result","content":"# SIGIL — Phase 0: the memory loop\n\nLocal-first total recall over your entire Claude history, exposed to every Claude session via\nan MCP memory server. Ask *\"what did I decide about X\"* → get a **cited** answer from a\ntamper-evident record of your own work. Offense-free by construction. (Full spec:\n`../Pictures/PENTEST-main/SIGIL.md`.)\n\n```\n~/.claude/projects/**.jsonl ─┐\ngit commits ─────────────────┼─ingest─▶ episodic spine ─embed─▶ vectors ─┐\nsubagent transcripts ────────┤        (append-only, hash-   (Qdrant       ├─▶ MCP memory server\ncurated memory/*.md ─────────┘         chained, signed)      server)      │    (8 gated, cited tools)\n                                             │                            │\n                                             └────rebuild────▶ Kùzu graph ─┘\n                                        consolidate (ARCHIVIST, agent-driven, gated) ─▶ facts\n```\n\n## What's built\n\n- **`sigil/spine/`** — append-only, hash-chained JSONL, the source of truth. Two-layer integrity:\n  a **binding** check (payload still hashes to its digest) + a **chain** check (links intact); the\n  signed head distinguishes benign growth from truncation/rewrite (**tampering**). Owner-signed\n  (Ed25519). 43k+ records.\n- **`sigil/reuse/`** — the integrity substrate (hash chain + Ed25519), **vendored verbatim** from\n  the owner's CRUCIBLE work. SIGIL imports **zero** `framework.*` modules; `assert_no_offense()`\n  enforces it at import in every SIGIL process.\n- **`sigil/ingest/`** — threaded Claude-Code JSONL (text/thinking/tool blocks) + **git commits**\n  (live post-commit/post-merge hooks) + **subagent transcripts** (each its own titled session) +\n  curated docs. Incremental per-file cursors; idempotent.\n- **`sigil/vectors/`** — on-device embeddings (fastembed / bge-small, CPU, no API, no cost) → Qdrant\n  **server mode** (concurrent serve + ingest). Honesty gate: an absent topic returns \"no grounded\n  match\", never a fabrication.\n- **`sigil/graph/`** — a **deterministic** Kùzu mirror of the spine (Project/Session/Document/Commit\n  + containment); rebuilt by replay with an atomic swap; every node cites its spine anchor. Two\n  rebuilds → identical node/edge sets.\n- **`sigil/consolidate/`** — the **ARCHIVIST** pass: agent-driven (`claude -p` on Max) extraction of\n  decisions/commitments/entities behind a **demote-only veracity gate** that *re-executes* every\n  citation (a fact is promoted only if its quote is verbatim in a cited spine record). Only\n  spine-traceable facts become records; the rest are recorded honestly as refusals, never dropped.\n- **`sigil/mcp/`** — **8 gated, read-only, cited MCP tools**: `memory_search`, `episodic_range`,\n  `ingest_status`, `graph_entity`, `graph_query`, `threads_open`, `commitments_due`,\n  `contradictions_pending`. Registered in Claude Code **and** Claude Desktop.\n\n## Use\n\n```bash\nV=~/.sigil/venv/bin/python\n$V -m sigil.cli ingest              # history + subagents (+ --git, --docs) → spine\n$V -m sigil.cli index               # spine → vectors (incremental)\n$V -m sigil.cli graph               # rebuild the deterministic entity graph\n$V -m sigil.cli sign                # anchor the spine (signed head)\n$V -m sigil.cli consolidate --provider heuristic   # offline extraction (no Max spend)\n$V -m sigil.cli consolidate --provider agent       # agent-driven (headless claude -p, Max)\n$V -m sigil.cli search \"the veracity firewall\"     # cited recall\n$V -m sigil.cli status              # counts + integrity\n```\n\nTests: `$V tests/test_integrity.py` · `test_graph.py` · `test_consolidate.py` (integrity /\ndeterminism / demote-only gate, all offline).\n\n===KERNEL-README===\n# SIGIL KERNEL — Phase 1 (KERNEL + WARDEN)\n\nThe Rust core of SIGIL: a **KERNEL** that routes intent through the cognition cascade, and\n**WARDEN**, the fail-closed authorization kernel every tool invocation crosses — tiered A0–A3\nand appended to a hash-chained, Ed25519-signed **action log** (the same construction as the\nPhase-0 episodic spine, pointed inward). Offense-free by doctrine: it orchestrates and gates;\nit never scans, exploits, or targets third parties.\n\n```\nintent ─▶ KERNEL ─▶ T0 router ─┬─ answer_local ─▶ memory (Phase-0 sigil CLI)\n(palette)                       ├─ escalate_T1 ──▶ claude -p  (fast: Haiku)\n                                ├─ escalate_T2 ──▶ claude -p  (deep: Sonnet)\n                                └─ dispatch_agent ▶ (agent mesh — Phase 3)\n                                        │\n        every tool invocation ─────────┴──▶ WARDEN: classify tier ─▶ gate ─▶ signed action log\n```\n\n## WARDEN — the authorization kernel (SIGIL §5)\n\n- **Tiers, fail-closed.** `A0` observe/answer (auto) · `A1` reversible internal write (auto+logged)\n  · `A2` external-visible (queued for one-tap) · `A3` destructive/financial/security (explicit,\n  no promotion). Classification is **token-based** (the tool name is split on `.`/`_`/`-` into\n  whole tokens): danger is checked first, `A0` is reachable only via a positive safe-verb\n  allowlist, and anything not positively classified — unknown tools, or a benign token inside a\n  dangerous word (`overwrite` ≠ `write`, `forget` ≠ `get`) — falls to **A3**. Secret/identity/\n  network/financial *targets* are A3 regardless of verb (`secrets.read`, `iam.policy.write`).\n- **Signed action spine.** Each record `{seq, ts, agent, tool, args_hash, tier, decision,\n  approver, result_hash, cert_digest, prev_hash, entry_hash, sig}` is Ed25519-signed AND\n  hash-chained. `ts` is bound into the digest (timestamps are tamper-evident). A separately-signed\n  **head** anchors the log's *length*, so tamper (binding), reorder/delete (chain), tail-truncation\n  or wipe (head), and forgery (signature) are all caught at the exact seq.\n- **Anti-rollback anchor.** A stateless local `verify` cannot detect a rollback to an *older\n  validly-signed head*. So each head `{count, head_hash}` is cross-checkpointed (Ed25519-signed by\n  the WARDEN key, scoped by pubkey) into the append-only, separately-signed, actively-growing\n  Phase-0 spine; `verify` **fails closed** if the on-disk head count is below the spine-anchored\n  high-water (rollback, exit 2) OR if the anchor is unreachable/spine-tampered (unverifiable,\n  exit 3 — `--allow-unanchored` is the explicit first-run/offline escape). Auto-anchors after every\n  A2/A3 attempt. *(Ultimate residual: a full wipe + fresh key changes the visible pubkey; only\n  hardware/remote monotonic state is absolute for a local audit log.)*\n- **Tier registry.** Per-tool pins in `~/.sigil/warden/tools.json`, applied **raise-only**\n  (`max(pin, inferred)`) so the file can make a tool more gated but never downgrade it.\n- **Self-audit (C18).** `sigil-kernel audit` replays the log verbatim; `verify` re-checks the\n  chain + every signature + the head + the anti-rollback anchor.\n\n## Cognition cascade (SIGIL §7)\n\n`T0` router (deterministic, rule-based v1 — a local Ollama model is a refinement) classifies every\ninput into {answer_local, escalate_T1, escalate_T2, dispatch_agent}. `T1`/`T2` run via headless\n`claude -p` on the Max plan.\n\n## Use\n\n```bash\ncargo build --release\nK=./target/release/sigil-kernel\n$K ask \"what did i decide about the graph database\"   # T0 → memory (A0), cited, logged\n$K do memory.write \"note: chose serve-the-quote\"      # A1 auto, logged\n$K do git.push origin main                            # A3 — BLOCKED, awaiting explicit approval\n$K checkpoint                                         # cross-anchor the head into the spine (anti-rollback)\n$K audit                                              # replay the signed action spine (C18)\n$K verify                                             # chain + signatures + head + anti-rollback anchor\n$K verify --allow-unanchored                          # weaker: local integrity only (first run/offline)\n$K pubkey                                             # the WARDEN public key\n```\n\nRuntime data: `~/.sigil/warden/` (`keys/warden.key` 0600, `actionlog.jsonl` + signed\n`actionlog.head.json`). `cargo test` runs the WARDEN test suite (crypto, tiers, action-log\nintegrity incl. tamper/reorder/tail-truncation/wipe/forged-key, router).\n\n## Not yet (later phases)\n\nGlobal hotkey + voice daemon (Phase 1.5/2), the real agent mesh (Phase 3), a local Ollama T0\nrouter model, and — for absolute anti-rollback — hardware/remote monotonic state (the local\nspine anchor raises the bar but a full wipe + fresh key is still a residual).","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"248c927a-8e9d-4c11-8acb-0b0467765245","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:39:30.038Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"aa129442-d97d-487a-b343-1bf48ad89420","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

set ELEVENLABS_API_KEY first\")\n214\t            return\n215\t        print(f\"  ElevenLabs voices matching {a.find_voice!r} — pin one with `sigil voice --set-voice <id>`:\")\n216\t        for v in voices:\n217\t            print(f\"    {v['voice_id']}  {str(v['name'])[:22]:<22} [{v.get('accent','')}/{v.get('category','')}]  {v.get('description','')}\")\n218\t        return\n219\t    if a.set_voice:\n220\t        set_voice(a.set_voice)\n221\t        print(f\"  pinned TTS voice → {a.set_voice} (SIGIL_TTS_VOICE_ID)\")\n222\t        return\n223\t    if a.mic:\n224\t        run_mic(asr=a.asr, wake=a.wake, tts=a.tts, tts_voice=a.tts_voice)\n225\t        return\n226\t    if not a.file:\n227\t        print(\"  need --file <wav> (file mode), --mic (live), --find-voice <q>, or --set-voice <id>\")\n228\t        return\n229\t    p = run_file(a.file, a.out, asr=a.asr, tts=a.tts, tts_voice=a.tts_voice)\n230\t    print(f\"  transcript: {p.transcript!r}\")\n231\t    print(f\"  response:   {p.response!r}\")\n232\t    print(f\"  spoke →     {a.out}\")\n233\t    print(f\"  events:     {p.events}\")\n234\t"}]},"uuid":"fd8b341d-6ecf-4829-9124-86a648a8d72f","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:39:41.677Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"ed668a66-9b83-4e20-a26c-929874f401d6","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
