{"parentUuid":"69e12bde-1d45-4b66-b8e3-38fc986373b4","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a9a9282aa914a9b3f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_019jGXYHCahFSNpG69ddSDxx","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t//! WARDEN — the authorization kernel (SIGIL §5). The single choke point every tool invocation\n2\t//! crosses: classify the tool into a tier, derive the fail-closed gate decision, and (after the\n3\t//! caller executes-or-blocks) append a signed, chained action-log record. WARDEN never executes\n4\t//! tools itself; it decides and it records.\n5\t\n6\tuse std::io;\n7\tuse std::path::Path;\n8\t\n9\tuse crate::actionlog::{ActionLog, ActionRecord};\n10\tuse crate::crypto::WardenKey;\n11\tuse crate::registry::Registry;\n12\tuse crate::tiers::{gate, Decision, Tier};\n13\t\n14\tpub struct Warden {\n15\t    log: ActionLog,\n16\t    key: WardenKey,\n17\t    registry: Registry,\n18\t}\n19\t\n20\tpub struct Verdict {\n21\t    pub tier: Tier,\n22\t    pub decision: Decision,\n23\t}\n24\t\n25\timpl Warden {\n26\t    /// Open (or initialize) WARDEN under `dir`: the signing key lives in `dir/keys`, the action\n27\t    /// log at `dir/actionlog.jsonl`.\n28\t    pub fn open(dir: &Path) -> io::Result<Self> {\n29\t        let key = WardenKey::load_or_create(&dir.join(\"keys\"))?;\n30\t        let log = ActionLog::new(dir.join(\"actionlog.jsonl\"));\n31\t        let registry = Registry::load(&dir.join(\"tools.json\"));\n32\t        Ok(Warden { log, key, registry })\n33\t    }\n34\t\n35\t    /// Classify + derive the gate decision (pure — no logging, no side effects). The tier is the\n36\t    /// raise-only registry result (an explicit pin can only make a tool MORE gated than inferred).\n37\t    pub fn decide(&self, tool: &str) -> Verdict {\n38\t        let tier = self.registry.tier(tool);\n39\t        Verdict { tier, decision: gate(tier) }\n40\t    }\n41\t\n42\t    /// Append a signed action-log record for a completed (or blocked) invocation.\n43\t    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]\n44\t    pub fn record(\n45\t        &self,\n46\t        agent: &str,\n47\t        tool: &str,\n48\t        args_hash: &str,\n49\t        verdict: &Verdict,\n50\t        approver: &str,\n51\t        result_hash: &str,\n52\t        ts_epoch: u64,\n53\t    ) -> io::Result<ActionRecord> {\n54\t        self.log.append(\n55\t            &self.key, agent, tool, args_hash,\n56\t            verdict.tier.as_str(), verdict.decision.as_str(),\n57\t            approver, result_hash, ts_epoch,\n58\t        )\n59\t    }\n60\t\n61\t    pub fn verify(&self) -> Result<usize, String> {\n62\t        self.log.verify(&self.key.public_hex())\n63\t    }\n64\t\n65\t    pub fn records(&self) -> io::Result<Vec<ActionRecord>> {\n66\t        self.log.records()\n67\t    }\n68\t\n69\t    /// The on-disk signed head as (count, head_hash) — for the anti-rollback anchor.\n70\t    pub fn head(&self) -> io::Result<Option<(u64, String)>> {\n71\t        self.log.head()\n72\t    }\n73\t\n74\t    pub fn public_hex(&self) -> String {\n75\t        self.key.public_hex()\n76\t    }\n77\t\n78\t    /// Sign a message with the WARDEN key (hex) — used to authenticate anchor-set to the spine.\n79\t    pub fn sign_hex(&self, msg: &[u8]) -> String {\n80\t        self.key.sign_hex(msg)\n81\t    }\n82\t}\n83\t\n84\t#[cfg(test)]\n85\tmod tests {\n86\t    use super::*;\n87\t    use crate::crypto::sha256_hex;\n88\t\n89\t    fn tmp_dir() -> std::path::PathBuf {\n90\t        let mut p = std::env::temp_dir();\n91\t        p.push(format!(\"sigil-warden-{}\", sha256_hex(format!(\"{:?}\", std::thread::current().id()).as_bytes())));\n92\t        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&p);\n93\t        p\n94\t    }\n95\t\n96\t    #[test]\n97\t    fn a0_a1_auto_run_a2_a3_blocked() {\n98\t        let w = Warden::open(&tmp_dir()).unwrap();\n99\t        assert!(w.decide(\"memory.search\").decision.may_run(), \"A0 must auto-run\");\n100\t        assert!(w.decide(\"memory.write\").decision.may_run(), \"A1 must auto-run\");\n101\t        assert!(!w.decide(\"email.send\").decision.may_run(), \"A2 must NOT auto-run\");\n102\t        assert!(!w.decide(\"git.push\").decision.may_run(), \"A3 must NOT auto-run\");\n103\t        assert!(!w.decide(\"unknown.thing\").decision.may_run(), \"unknown must fail closed\");\n104\t    }\n105\t\n106\t    #[test]\n107\t    fn recorded_actions_verify_and_survive_reopen() {\n108\t        let dir = tmp_dir();\n109\t        {\n110\t            let w = Warden::open(&dir).unwrap();\n111\t            let v = w.decide(\"memory.search\");\n112\t            w.record(\"KERNEL\", \"memory.search\", \"b3:aa\", &v, \"auto\", \"b3:res\", 1000).unwrap();\n113\t        }\n114\t        // reopen (loads the same persisted key) and append + verify\n115\t        let w2 = Warden::open(&dir).unwrap();\n116\t        let v = w2.decide(\"email.send\");\n117\t        w2.record(\"ENVOY\", \"email.send\", \"b3:bb\", &v, \"none\", \"blocked:queued\", 1001).unwrap();\n118\t        assert_eq!(w2.verify().unwrap(), 2, \"the whole log must verify under the persisted key\");\n119\t    }\n120\t}\n121\t"}]},"uuid":"e9adc51c-6b5c-4d51-9a67-a9b84275693e","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:39:42.206Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"69e12bde-1d45-4b66-b8e3-38fc986373b4","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"987c2f96-90be-4ce6-ba08-ad412674450e","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a9a9282aa914a9b3f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_012P5JBBkGUWpv1QY89z8PUw","type":"tool_result","content":"16:3. **Local-first.** Perception, memory, and routing run on hardware you own. Frontier inference is a tool SIGIL calls, not a place your life is stored.\n29:                        │  voice · hotkey palette · mobile     │\n34:│   PERCEPTION    │───▶│         KERNEL (orchestrator)        │\n71:| C4 | Screen understanding | \"What am I looking at, what's the error, summarize this thread\" | Accessibility tree + screenshot → VLM; hotkey-triggered | KERNEL | A0 |\n72:| C5 | Camera vision, on demand | Frame capture → description, OCR, object/state queries | v4l2/ffmpeg grab → frontier VLM via MCP tool | SENTINEL | A0 |\n73:| C6 | Ambient vision (opt-in) | Change detection in the room; escalation on events | 0.5 fps local small VLM (moondream-class) → escalate frame to frontier VLM on delta | SENTINEL | A0 |\n79:| C12 | Communications drafting + triage | Inbox triage, reply drafts, outreach follow-up tracking (UNDP, ANTIC, customs, partners) | ENVOY: IMAP/SMTP + WhatsApp bridge; drafts queue for approval; relationship state lives in graph | ENVOY | A2 |\n131:### 4.3 SENTINEL (perception & monitoring)\n156:- **Tools:** IMAP/SMTP, WhatsApp bridge, contact graph.\n194:- **Kill switch:** single command halts the agent mesh, leaves perception + memory read alive.\n217:| WhatsApp | Export import (manual) or bridge | As available |\n249:| T3 | Specialists | On demand | VLM (vision), embedding model, TTS/ASR | Path-dependent |\n255:## 8. Perception Subsystem\n258:- **Vision:** on-demand capture (C5) default. Ambient (C6) is opt-in per session, indicator-lit, frames processed locally, only escalated frames leave the machine, nothing persisted beyond event records.\n259:- **Screen:** hotkey → accessibility tree + screenshot → VLM. {"parentUuid":"a31e67e9-752a-436a-8a10-8225f70183ce","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a9a9282aa914a9b3f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01VB6YXZt61WZL3n6pvzWHoh","type":"tool_result","content":"===sentinel head===\n\"\"\"SENTINEL (SIGIL §4.3) — perception & monitoring: see everything, report only what matters.\nCeiling A1 (writes only event records). The failure mode is NOISE, so SENTINEL applies a\nSALIENCE threshold and an ALERT BUDGET: at most `alert_budget` events per run, highest-salience\nfirst; the rest are suppressed (and counted). Watchers are pluggable — the built-ins read the\nspine + local system; live watchers (IMAP IDLE, CalDAV, uptime probes) are optional additions.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport shutil\nfrom typing import List, Protocol, runtime_checkable\n\nfrom .base import Agent, AgentResult, Proposal, Tier\n\n\n@runtime_checkable\nclass Watcher(Protocol):\n    def poll(self) -> List[dict]: ...   # each: {kind, summary, salience(0..1), detail?}\n\n\nclass SpineActivityWatcher:\n    \"\"\"Salient signals derived from the spine: a burst of new commits, and any pending\n    self-contradiction the consolidation flagged.\"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, store, since_seq: int, commit_burst: int = 3):\n        self.store, self.since_seq, self.commit_burst = store, since_seq, commit_burst\n\n    def poll(self) -> List[dict]:\n        out: List[dict] = []\n        commits = sum(1 for r in self.store.iter_records(since_seq=self.since_seq) if r.kind == \"commit\")\n        if commits >= self.commit_burst:\n            out.append({\"kind\": \"commits\", \"summary\": f\"{commits} new commits since last check\",\n                        \"salience\": min(1.0, 0.4 + commits / 20.0)})\n===killswitch head===\n\"\"\"The kill switch (SIGIL §5) — a single latch that halts the agent mesh while leaving perception and\nmemory-read (A0 observe) alive. State is append-only on the spine.\n\nASYMMETRIC AUTHENTICATION (Phase 6 red-pen RP-2): halting is always the safe direction, so ANY\nengage event halts (a nuisance forged-engage is at worst a fail-safe DoS). UN-halting is the\ndangerous direction, so a RELEASE is honored ONLY if it is signed by the owner key and verifies\nagainst the trusted pubkey — a forged release can never revive a halted mesh. Read fresh per decision\nso a release (or engage) takes effect immediately.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nfrom .authn import signed_payload, verify_signed\nfrom .identity import owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n\nSIGNAL = \"governor.killswitch\"\n_CORE = (\"signal\", \"state\")\n\n\nclass KillSwitch:\n    def __init__(self, store, *, owner_key=None, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None):\n        self.store = store\n        self.owner_key = owner_key if owner_key is not None else owner_keypair()\n        self.trusted_pubkey = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n\n    def engage(self, *, by: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n        core = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"state\": \"engaged\"}\n        payload = {**signed_payload(core, self.owner_key), \"by\": by, \"reason\": reason,\n                   \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"WARDEN\", payload=payload)\n\n    def release(self, *, by: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n        core = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"state\": \"released\"}\n        payload = {**signed_payload(core, self.owner_key), \"by\": by, \"reason\": reason,\n                   \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"WARDEN\", payload=payload)\n\n    def is_engaged(self) -> bool:\n        engaged = False\n        for r in self.store.iter_records():","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"9d36152c-254d-4715-b26c-0c1b9675f0fe","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:40:01.780Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a31e67e9-752a-436a-8a10-8225f70183ce","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"c900b2f4-d76f-4364-9d54-a65803185b59","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a9a9282aa914a9b3f","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011Cd9rUY7BiSYEFZvd5feYc","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"I have now mapped the full substrate. Here is the structured report.\n\n---\n\n# SIGIL substrate map — perception, providers, config, voice, kernel, bridge seams\n\nRepo: `/home/kali/sigil` — Python package `sigil/`, Rust core `kernel/`. Phase status (from `README.md` / `kernel/README.md`): Phase 0 memory, Phase 1 KERNEL+WARDEN, Phase 2 voice, Phase 3 agent mesh, Phase 5 perception, Phase 6 hardening. Perception, voice, and agent providers are all built against injected Protocols with an inert \"real\" seam + a deterministic test double — the exact pattern both goals slot into.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Perception subsystem — the VLM seams\n\n### `sigil/perception/capture.py`\n- **`Frame`** — `@dataclass(frozen=True)`: `kind:str` (\"screen\"|\"camera\"), `sha256:str`, `text:str` (OCR/accessibility = ground truth), `image_path:Optional[str]`, `width/height:int`. Classmethod `Frame.from_image(cls, kind, image_path, *, text=\"\")` hashes the bytes.\n- **OS-specific screenshot backends** — a hardcoded Linux tool list, tried in order:\n  ```python\n  _SCREENSHOT_TOOLS = ((\"scrot\",[\"scrot\",\"-o\"]),(\"gnome-screenshot\",[\"gnome-screenshot\",\"-f\"]),\n                       (\"spectacle\",[\"spectacle\",\"-b\",\"-n\",\"-o\"]),(\"import\",[\"import\",\"-window\",\"root\"]))\n  ```\n- **`grab_screen() -> Optional[Frame]`** — loops `_SCREENSHOT_TOOLS`, `shutil.which(name)` gates each; `tempfile.mktemp(suffix=\".png\")`; returns `Frame.from_image(\"screen\", out, text=_ocr(out))` or `None`. The accessibility-tree path (richer, \"C4\") is called a \"documented seam, not wired\".\n- **`grab_camera(device=\"/dev/video0\") -> Optional[Frame]`** — Linux v4l2 via ffmpeg: `[\"ffmpeg\",\"-y\",\"-f\",\"v4l2\",\"-i\",device,\"-frames:v\",\"1\",out]`; gated on `shutil.which(\"ffmpeg\")` and `Path(device).exists()`.\n- **`_ocr(image_path) -> str`** — tesseract only: `subprocess.run([\"tesseract\", image_path, \"stdout\"], …)`; `\"\"` if tesseract absent (an absent OCR is not a failure).\n- **`StaticFrame(*, kind=\"screen\", text=\"\", tag=\"\") -> Frame`** — deterministic double, `sha256_hex((tag or text).encode())`, no filesystem; used to drive `ambient_watch` change-detection in tests.\n\n### `sigil/perception/vision.py` — the VLM plug point (inert)\n```python\n@runtime_checkable\nclass VisionModel(Protocol):\n    def describe(self, frame: Frame, question: str) -> str: ...   # advisory free-text reading\n\nclass ClaudeVision:\n    def __init__(self, endpoint: Optional[str]=None, model: str=\"claude-sonnet-5\", timeout: int=60): ...\n    def describe(self, frame: Frame, question: str) -> str:\n        if not self.endpoint or not frame.image_path:\n            return \"\"   # no endpoint / no image → no reading (honest empty, not a guess)\n        # A wired implementation would POST the base64 image + question to self.endpoint here.\n        return \"\"\n```\nThis is the single seam for GOAL A. The docstring states the intent verbatim: *\"a real VLM call needs either the metered Anthropic vision API or a local moondream-class model; neither is wired offline here.\"* Note `model=\"claude-sonnet-5\"` is the default; the actual POST is a comment at line 33.\n\n### `sigil/perception/perceive.py` — where a `VisionModel` is injected\n- **`Perceptor(Agent)`**: `name=\"PERCEPTION\"`, `ceiling=Tier.A1`.\n- **`perceive(self, question: str, frame: Frame, *, vision: Optional[VisionModel]=None) -> AgentResult`** — the injection point:\n  ```python\n  reading = \"\"\n  if vision is not None:\n      try: reading = vision.describe(frame, question) or \"\"\n      except Exception: reading = \"\"       # a failing VLM yields no reading, never a crash/guess\n  text = compose_perception(question, frame, reading)\n  ```\n  Emits one `Proposal(\"event\", {...}, Tier.A0)` carrying both `\"captured_text\"` (authoritative) and `\"vision_reading_advisory\"` (the VLM output), plus `\"grounded\": bool(frame.text.strip())`.\n- **`compose_perception(question, frame, reading) -> str`** — \"serve-the-quote\": captured OCR text rendered under `AUTHORITATIVE_HEADER` at column 0, each captured line guard-prefixed `\"  │ \"` so screen content can't forge the header boundary; the VLM reading goes under `ADVISORY_HEADER` (\"a guess about the image, NOT verified\"). This is doctrine: the VLM never becomes the source of truth.\n- **`ambient_watch(self, frames: List[Frame], *, vision=None) -> tuple`** — the C6 opt-in loop: establishes a baseline frame, escalates (`self.perceive(...)`) **only** when `fr.sha256 != last.sha256`. Docstring explicitly flags the byte-identity delta as the place a \"perceptual-hash / small-VLM delta\" (the moondream-class local model) swaps in.\n\n### CLI wiring — `sigil/cli.py` (≈185–203, `cmd_agents`, action `\"perceive\"`)\n```python\nif a.image:   frame = Frame.from_image(\"screen\", a.image)\nelif a.camera: frame = grab_camera()\nelse:          frame = grab_screen()\n...\nres = Perceptor(store).perceive(a.question or \"\", frame)\n```\nLoad-bearing gap: **the CLI calls `perceive(...)` with no `vision=` argument**, so no VLM runs today. Injecting a real `VisionModel` here (and/or as a `Perceptor.perceive` default) is the concrete wire-in for GOAL A. `ClaudeVision` is never instantiated anywhere outside `__init__.py`'s export; tests inject a `FabricatingVision()` double (`tests/test_perception_bastion.py`).\n\n---\n\n## 2. Provider-abstraction precedents to mirror for a VLM\n\nAll three follow the identical shape: a `@runtime_checkable Protocol` + a `Claude*`/`*Provider` real impl that shells `claude -p` + a deterministic double. The **consolidation trio is the closest precedent to GOAL A's cascade** (frontier API + local fallback).\n\n| File | Protocol | Real provider(s) | Transport |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `sigil/agents/artificer.py` | `Coder.code(task, workdir)->str` | `ClaudeCoder` | `claude -p <prompt> --permission-mode acceptEdits`, `cwd=workdir`, `timeout=600` |\n| `sigil/agents/scholar.py` | `Synthesizer.synthesize(question, docs)->List[dict]` | `ClaudeSynthesizer` | `claude -p <prompt> --model <FAST_MODEL>` |\n| `sigil/consolidate/extract.py` | `ExtractionProvider.extract(records)->list[CandidateFact]` | `AgentProvider`, `ApiProvider`, `LocalProvider`, `ReplayProvider`, `HeuristicProvider` | see below |\n\n**`ClaudeCoder`** (`artificer.py`): `claude_bin=\"/home/kali/.local/bin/claude\"` hardcoded; `subprocess.run([self.claude_bin,\"-p\",prompt,\"--permission-mode\",\"acceptEdits\"], cwd=workdir, …)`.\n\n**`ClaudeSynthesizer`** (`scholar.py`): `claude_bin=\"/home/kali/.local/bin/claude\"`, `model=\"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001\"`; `subprocess.run([self.claude_bin,\"-p\",prompt,\"--model\",self.model], …)`.\n\n**The `sigil/consolidate/extract.py` cascade** — this is the reference model for \"Claude API + local fallback, cascade doctrine\":\n- **`AgentProvider`** (`--provider claude`): headless `claude -p` on the Max plan, zero API cost. `cmd = [self.claude_bin,\"-p\",build_prompt(records)]` + `[\"--model\", self.model]`.\n- **`ApiProvider`** (`--provider api`): **the only real Anthropic HTTP path in the repo.** Raw `urllib.request` POST to `https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages`, headers `{\"x-api-key\": self.api_key, \"anthropic-version\":\"2023-06-01\"}`, key from `SIGIL_ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` / `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`. No SDK. This is the exact template for a `ClaudeVision.describe` that POSTs a base64 image block.\n- **`LocalProvider`** (`--provider local`): **the only Ollama usage in the repo.** `urllib` POST to `{OLLAMA_HOST default http://127.0.0.1:11434}/api/generate`, `model=\"llama3.1\"`. This is the template for a local moondream-class VLM fallback.\n- Shared helpers to reuse: `build_prompt(records)`, `parse_candidates(raw, *, extractor)` (tolerant JSON/```json extraction).\n- CLI (`cli.py` ~370): `--provider` choices `claude|api|local` — the doctrine string is *\"claude=Max plan (claude -p); api=Anthropic API key; local=Ollama\"*.\n\n**How SIGIL shells to `claude -p`:** always `subprocess.run` with a fixed absolute `/home/kali/.local/bin/claude`, `-p <prompt>`, optional `--model`, `capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=…`; failures degrade to `[]`/`\"\"` (never fabricate). The kernel does the same in Rust (`kernel/src/cascade.rs`, §5).\n\n**Anthropic API path:** only `ApiProvider` (above). **Ollama:** only `LocalProvider`. No other local-model usage.\n\n---\n\n## 3. Config & paths — `sigil/config.py`\n\n- **`SIGIL_HOME = Path(os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_HOME\", ~/.sigil))`** — data root, chmod 0700.\n- **`_load_env_file()`** (runs at import): reads `SIGIL_HOME/\"sigil.env\"` line-by-line `KEY=VALUE`, `os.environ.setdefault(k, v)` — *\"The real environment always wins (setdefault).\"* This is the whole secrets story.\n- **Path constants:** `SPINE_PATH = SIGIL_HOME/\"spine\"/\"spine.jsonl\"`, `HEAD_PATH`, `KEYS_DIR = …/spine/keys`, `CACHE_DIR`, `QDRANT_PATH`, `GRAPH_DIR`, plus `CLAUDE_PROJECTS = ~/.claude/projects`, `PROJECT_ALLOWLIST` (default `-home-kali-Pictures-PENTEST-main`).\n- Vector/identity env: `SIGIL_QDRANT_URL`, `SIGIL_QDRANT_COLLECTION`, `EMBED_MODEL=BAAI/bge-small-en-v1.5`, `SCOPE`, `OWNER_KEY_ID`.\n- `ensure_dirs()` mkdirs + `os.chmod(d, 0o700)`.\n\n**Secrets handling — OS-specific note:** there is **no OS keyring/keychain integration anywhere in Python**. Every API key (ElevenLabs, Anthropic) is read from env or the plaintext file `~/.sigil/sigil.env`:\n- ElevenLabs: `os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_ELEVENLABS_API_KEY\") or os.environ.get(\"ELEVENLABS_API_KEY\")` (`voice/backends.py`).\n- `set_voice(voice_id)` (`voice/backends.py`) is the only writer: it rewrites `SIGIL_HOME/\"sigil.env\"` persisting `SIGIL_TTS_VOICE_ID=…`.\n- Cryptographic keys are files, not keyring: WARDEN Ed25519 key at `~/.sigil/warden/keys/warden.key` (0600, created by Rust `crypto::WardenKey::load_or_create`); owner key via `sigil/governor/identity.py`.\n- Ironically, `kernel/src/tiers.rs` classifies the *tokens* `vault`, `keyring`, `keychain`, `keystore`, `hsm` as A3 dangerous targets, but nothing in the codebase actually stores secrets in any of them. Paths are all hardcoded absolute Linux (`/home/kali/...`); `SIGIL_HOME` is the only portability lever.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Voice subsystem — the \"real cloud API + daemon loop\" precedent\n\n### `sigil/voice/backends.py`\n- **`ElevenLabsTts`** (cloud TTS, owner's choice): `__init__(voice_id=None, model_id=\"eleven_flash_v2_5\", api_key=None, timeout=30)`; voice_id defaults to `SIGIL_TTS_VOICE_ID` else stock \"Rachel\". `synth(text)` → `urllib.request` POST to `https://api.elevenlabs.io/v1/text-to-speech/{voice_id}/stream?output_format=pcm_16000`, headers `{\"xi-api-key\": self.api_key, …}`, streams pcm through `_FrameBuffer`. Raises if no key.\n- **`ElevenLabsAsr`** (\"Scribe\" cloud STT): `transcribe(frames)` assembles an in-memory 16 kHz WAV, hand-rolls `multipart/form-data`, POSTs `https://api.elevenlabs.io/v1/speech-to-text`.\n- **`find_voices(query=\"jarvis\", …)`** / **`set_voice(voice_id)`** — search shared voice library, persist chosen id to `sigil.env`.\n- Local/zero-dep tiers alongside: `EnergyVad`, `EnergyWake`, `FileAudioSource`/`FileSink`, `SilenceTts` (always available), and lazy-imported ML: `WhisperAsr` (faster-whisper), `SileroVad`, `OpenWakeWord`, `PiperTts` (sovereign local TTS), `MicAudioSource`/`SpeakerSink` (sounddevice). This is the same \"cloud API + local fallback\" cascade GOAL A wants, already realized for audio.\n\n### `sigil/voice/run.py` — the driver + fallback selection\n- `_make_asr(kind)` / `_make_tts(kind, voice)`: if `kind==\"elevenlabs\"` and no key → print + fall back to stub/`SilenceTts` (graceful degrade pattern).\n- **`run_file(wav_in, wav_out, *, asr=\"auto\", tts=\"silence\", tts_voice=None)`** — offline path.\n- **`run_mic(*, asr=\"elevenlabs\", wake=\"energy\", tts=\"elevenlabs\", tts_voice=None)`** — the live daemon loop: builds `VoicePipeline`, `p.run(MicAudioSource().frames())` inside `try/except KeyboardInterrupt`, `finally: sink.close()`. This is the run-loop template for a mobile/bridge daemon.\n\n### `sigil/voice/pipeline.py` — the full-duplex state machine\n`VoicePipeline(vad, wake, asr, tts, sink, dispatch, *, silence_frames=25, …, barge_in_frames=4)`. States `IDLE→LISTENING→THINKING→SPEAKING→IDLE`; frame-driven `on_frame`; barge-in with hysteresis; `run(frames)` pumps any iterable (mic or file) then `drain()`. Components are Protocols in `sigil/voice/components.py` (`Vad/WakeWord/Asr/Tts/AudioSink/Dispatch`) with deterministic mocks. Module note: live mic+speaker needs AEC/ducking — a runtime concern.\n\n### `sigil/voice/dispatch.py` — **the existing Python→Rust-kernel bridge**\n`KernelDispatch.send(text)` shells the kernel: `subprocess.run([self.kernel_bin, \"ask\", text], …)`, kernel_bin auto-resolved to `kernel/target/{release,debug}/sigil-kernel`. Strips `[T0…]/[WARDEN…]/[BLOCKED…]` status lines; a `[BLOCKED` line returns *\"that action needs your approval — it wasn't run.\"* This is the reusable \"recognized-text → authorized KERNEL path\" seam — a Telegram/WhatsApp bot would call the exact same `KernelDispatch.send`.\n\n---\n\n## 5. Rust kernel — `kernel/src/`\n\nModules (`lib.rs`): `actionlog`, `anchor`, `cascade`, `crypto`, `memory`, `registry`, `router`, `tiers`, `warden` (+ `main.rs`).\n\n- **`main.rs`** — a **clap CLI, not a resident daemon.** Subcommands: `Ask{intent}`, `Do{tool,args}`, `Audit`, `Verify{--allow-unanchored}`, `Checkpoint`, `Pubkey`. `cmd_ask` → `route(intent)` → maps to a tool name → `w.decide(tool)` (WARDEN) → if `may_run()` executes (`memory::search` / `cascade::run FAST|DEEP` / dispatch note), then `finish()` writes a signed action-log record; else `block()`. `warden_dir()` reads `SIGIL_HOME` env (fallback `$HOME/.sigil`) — the one portability lever, same as Python.\n- **`cascade.rs`** — **how the kernel shells to claude:** `const CLAUDE: &str = \"/home/kali/.local/bin/claude\";`, `FAST=\"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001\"`, `DEEP=\"claude-sonnet-5\"`; `run(prompt, model)` = `Command::new(CLAUDE).args([\"-p\", prompt, \"--model\", model]).output()`. Output is advisory; external effects still cross WARDEN.\n- **`router.rs`** — deterministic rule-based T0 (`Route::{AnswerLocal,EscalateT1,EscalateT2,DispatchAgent}`), keyword cue lists, no model call. Docstring: a local Ollama model is a later refinement.\n- **`tiers.rs`** — token-based A0–A3 classifier, fail-closed to A3. Relevant to GOAL B: **`A2_TOKENS` already includes `\"notify\"`, `\"webhook\"`, `\"sms\"`, `\"message\"`, `\"post\"`, `\"send\"`** (external-visible → queued), and **`A3_TOKENS` includes `\"keyring\"`, `\"keychain\"`, `\"keystore\"`, `\"vault\"`, `\"secret(s)\"`, `\"token(s)\"`**. So a future mobile-notify/webhook tool would auto-classify A2 (queued for approval) — the tiering seam for remote actions already exists, only the executor is missing.\n- **`warden.rs`** — `Warden::open(dir)` loads key + `ActionLog` + `Registry`; `decide(tool)->Verdict`, `record(...)`, `verify()`, `head()`, `sign_hex()`. Never executes tools.\n- **`memory.rs`** — **the existing Rust→Python bridge:** `const PY: &str = \"/home/kali/.sigil/venv/bin/python\";`, `Command::new(PY).arg(\"-m\").arg(\"sigil.cli\").args(args)`. Exposes `search(query,k)` and `status()`. Read-only from the kernel side.\n\n**Existing kernel↔mesh IPC (both directions, both subprocess over stdio — no socket/RPC):**\n- Rust → Python: `memory.rs` shells `python -m sigil.cli …`.\n- Python → Rust: `voice/dispatch.py` shells `sigil-kernel ask …`.\n- No shared socket, no HTTP, no long-lived channel. The \"agent mesh (ARTIFICER/SCHOLAR/ENVOY)\" dispatch route in `main.rs` currently just records the intent as a note (Phase 3 not wired into the Rust path). **Nothing OS-specific in the Rust beyond the two hardcoded absolute binary paths and `SIGIL_HOME`; no `#[cfg(target_os)]` anywhere.**\n\n---\n\n## 6. WireGuard / Telegram / WhatsApp / HTTP-bridge / notifications — confirmed absent\n\nA repo-wide search (`.py`, `.rs`, `.md`, `.toml`, excluding `target/`, `.git/`) for `wireguard|telegram|whatsapp|webhook|ntfy|fcm|apns|http.server|flask|fastapi|uvicorn|aiohttp|websocket|remote-approval` found **no implementation code**. `requirements.txt` has no web-server / bot / HTTP-client / WireGuard dependency (only cryptography, fastembed, kuzu, mcp, onnxruntime, pydantic, qdrant-client). No `sys.platform` / `platform.system` / `os.name` / `#[cfg(target_os)]` anywhere — there is **no cross-platform branching at all today; the codebase is single-target Linux.**\n\nWhat *does* exist as concrete reuse points for GOAL B:\n\n- **The remote-approval core is already built and hardened — `sigil/agents/approvals.py`.** `ApprovalQueue.approve(seq, approver, reason)` / `deny(...)` write an Ed25519 owner-signed resolution; `pending(store, trusted_pubkey_b64)` lists unresolved A2/A3 items; `verify_approval(record, trusted_pubkey)` is fail-closed. The module docstring names the exact intended seam:\n  > *\"This records the DECISION; it executes no external effect… The transport (Telegram/WhatsApp over WireGuard, §9.3) is a seam over this authenticated core.\"*\n  A phone bot is a transport that calls `pending()` to render the queue and `approve/deny()` to resolve — the auth (owner Ed25519 signature) is already the gate, so a remote surface cannot forge approvals (RP-APPROVAL-2/4 hardening).\n- **`ApprovalQueue` is surfaced in the CLI** (`cli.py` ~309, `cmd_approve`, subparsers `approve`/`deny` by seq) — a bot can shell the same CLI just as `voice/dispatch.py` shells the kernel.\n- **The kill switch** (`sigil/governor/killswitch.py`): `engage()` accepts any signal (fail-safe), `release()` requires a verified owner signature — a natural \"remote panic + remote resume\" surface for a phone.\n- **Notification tiering** already lives in `kernel/src/tiers.rs` (`notify`/`webhook`/`sms` = A2 queued) but there is **no push/notification executor** — no code emits to a phone. This is a pure greenfield seam; the tier classifier would gate it automatically.\n- **Command entry point for a bot** is settled: recognized text → `KernelDispatch.send(text)` → `sigil-kernel ask` (voice already proves this path). A Telegram/WhatsApp bot bridging \"phone as remote control to KERNEL\" reuses `KernelDispatch` verbatim.\n\n**Design intent (not code)** lives in the spec `/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main/SIGIL.md`: line 268 *\"Mobile bridge — Telegram/WhatsApp bot bridging to KERNEL over WireGuard… including approval taps for A2/A3 queue\"*; C5/C6 (lines 72–73) call for a frontier VLM via MCP tool + a 0.5 fps local moondream-class VLM for ambient delta; C12 (line 79) an ENVOY WhatsApp bridge. These are documented seams only — no corresponding code.\n\n---\n\n### Concrete reuse summary\n- **GOAL A (VLM):** implement `ClaudeVision.describe` (or a sibling `LocalVision`) by copying the `ApiProvider`/`LocalProvider` urllib pattern from `sigil/consolidate/extract.py`; inject the chosen `VisionModel` at the un-wired `Perceptor.perceive(...)` call in `sigil/cli.py:199` and into `ambient_watch`'s delta step. The advisory/authoritative discipline in `compose_perception` is already enforced.\n- **GOAL B (cross-platform + mobile):** the OS-branching layer does not exist yet — `capture.py` (Linux tools), `voice/backends.py` mic/speaker, and secrets (`config.py` plaintext `sigil.env`, no keyring) are the three single-target surfaces. The mobile bridge has a ready authenticated core (`ApprovalQueue` + owner Ed25519 identity), a ready command path (`KernelDispatch` → `sigil-kernel ask`), and ready tiering (`tiers.rs` A2 `notify/webhook/sms`), but zero transport/notification/WireGuard code."}],"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3121,"cache_read_input_tokens":87500,"output_tokens":10629,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3121},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":10629,"cache_read_input_tokens":87500,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3121,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3121,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011Cd9rUVyCaRAhRJszAcTXf","attributionAgent":"Explore","type":"assistant","uuid":"a9fcbd85-93dd-4215-8b71-a41c6ffedfee","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:42:15.745Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
